     1→import { BaseAbility } from './BaseAbility';
     2→import type { GameSceneAPI } from './types';
     3→import { LEGACY_PARAMS } from '../config/abilityParams';
     4→
     5→/**
     6→ * 레거시 (UR) — 피버 + 레거시 모드
     7→ * ★0: 6초 피버 / 불태우기 30%/2개(일반) 60%/4개(레거시) / 500점마다 / 10초 / 1.2배
     8→ * ★1: 8초 / 35%/2개 65%/4개 / 450점마다 / 10초 / 1.25배
     9→ * ★2: 10초 / 40%/3개 70%/4개 / 400점마다 / 12초 / 1.30배
    10→ * ★3: 12초 / 45%/3개 75%/4개 / 350점마다 / 15초 / 1.35배 + 모드 종료 직후 3초 미니 피버
    11→ */
    12→export class LegacyAbility extends BaseAbility {
    13→  private startFeverActive = false;
    14→  private legacyModeActive = false;
    15→  private accum           = 0;
    16→  private lastLegacyScore = 0;
    17→
    18→  private legacyTopGlow?:       Phaser.GameObjects.Graphics;
    19→  private legacyPulseTween?:    Phaser.Tweens.Tween;
    20→  private legacyRainTimer?:     Phaser.Time.TimerEvent;
    21→  private legacyEndTimer?:      Phaser.Time.TimerEvent; // activateLegacyMode 종료 delayedCall
    22→  private startFeverTimer?:     Phaser.Time.TimerEvent; // startFeverRain addEvent
    23→  private startFeverEndTimer?:  Phaser.Time.TimerEvent; // onCreate 피버 종료 delayedCall
    24→
    25→  // A: 상시 불꽃 오라
    26→  private oraTimer = 0;
    27→  private static readonly ORA_INTERVAL_NORMAL = 180; // ms
    28→  private static readonly ORA_INTERVAL_LEGACY =  80; // ms
    29→  private static readonly ORA_COLS_NORMAL = [0xff3300, 0xff6600, 0xffaa00];
    30→  private static readonly ORA_COLS_LEGACY = [0xffaa00, 0xffcc00, 0xffee44, 0xffffff];
    31→
    32→  // D: 빗줄기 색상
    33→  private static readonly RAIN_COLS_FEVER  = [0xff8800, 0xffaa00, 0xffcc00, 0xffee00];
    34→  private static readonly RAIN_COLS_LEGACY = [0xff5500, 0xff8800, 0xffaa00, 0xffcc00];
    35→
    36→  // ── 불꽃 물방울 다각형 좌표 ─────────────────────────────────────────
    37→  private static flamePts(w: number, h: number) {
    38→    return [
    39→      { x:  0,         y: -h        },
    40→      { x:  w * 0.55,  y: -h * 0.1  },
    41→      { x:  w * 0.75,  y:  h * 0.35 },
    42→      { x:  w * 0.3,   y:  h        },
    43→      { x: -w * 0.3,   y:  h        },
    44→      { x: -w * 0.75,  y:  h * 0.35 },
    45→      { x: -w * 0.55,  y: -h * 0.1  },
    46→    ];
    47→  }
    48→
    49→  // ── 8각 별 좌표 (빗줄기 공용) ────────────────────────────────────────
    50→  private static starPts(s: number) {
    51→    return [
    52→      { x:  0,       y: -s       }, { x:  s * 0.4, y: -s * 0.4 },
    53→      { x:  s,       y:  0       }, { x:  s * 0.4, y:  s * 0.4 },
    54→      { x:  0,       y:  s       }, { x: -s * 0.4, y:  s * 0.4 },
    55→      { x: -s,       y:  0       }, { x: -s * 0.4, y: -s * 0.4 },
    56→    ];
    57→  }
    58→
    59→  // ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    60→  // 생명주기
    61→  // ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    62→
    63→  override onCreate(api: GameSceneAPI): void {
    64→    this.startFeverActive = true;
    65→    this.startFeverRain(api);
    66→    this.startFeverEndTimer = api.scene.time.delayedCall(LEGACY_PARAMS.feverDuration, () => {
    67→      this.startFeverActive = false;
    68→    });
    69→  }
    70→
    71→  override onUpdate(api: GameSceneAPI): void {
    72→    const delta = api.scene.game.loop.delta;
    73→    this.oraTimer += delta;
    74→    const interval = this.legacyModeActive
    75→      ? LegacyAbility.ORA_INTERVAL_LEGACY
    76→      : LegacyAbility.ORA_INTERVAL_NORMAL;
    77→    if (this.oraTimer >= interval) {
    78→      this.oraTimer -= interval;
    79→      this.spawnOraFlame(api);
    80→    }
    81→  }
    82→
    83→  override getTickScore(base: number): number {
    84→    if (!this.legacyModeActive) return base;
    85→    this.accum += base * LEGACY_PARAMS.scoreExtra;
    86→    const bonus = Math.floor(this.accum);
    87→    this.accum -= bonus;
    88→    return base + bonus;
    89→  }
    90→
    91→  override overrideSpawnPoop(api: GameSceneAPI): boolean {
    92→    if (this.startFeverActive) {
    93→      api.spawnGoldPoop();
    94→      api.spawnGoldPoop();
    95→      return true;
    96→    }
    97→    return false;
    98→  }
    99→
   100→  override onAfterSpawnPoop(api: GameSceneAPI): void {
   101→    const chance = this.legacyModeActive ? LEGACY_PARAMS.burnChanceLegacy : LEGACY_PARAMS.burnChanceNormal;
   102→    const count  = this.legacyModeActive ? LEGACY_PARAMS.burnCountLegacy  : LEGACY_PARAMS.burnCountNormal;
   103→    if (Math.random() < chance) {
   104→      this.burnRandomPoops(api, count);
   105→    }
   106→  }
   107→
   108→  override onScoreMilestone(score: number, api: GameSceneAPI): void {
   109→    if (score % LEGACY_PARAMS.legacyInterval === 0 && score > this.lastLegacyScore) {
   110→      this.lastLegacyScore = score;
   111→      this.activateLegacyMode(api);
   112→    }
   113→  }
   114→
   115→  // ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
   116→  // A: 상시 불꽃 오라
   117→  // ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
   118→
   119→  private spawnOraFlame(api: GameSceneAPI): void {
   120→    const scene = api.scene;
   121→    const px    = api.player.x;
   122→    const py    = api.player.y;
   123→    const leg   = this.legacyModeActive;
   124→    const cols  = leg ? LegacyAbility.ORA_COLS_LEGACY : LegacyAbility.ORA_COLS_NORMAL;
   125→    const count = leg ? Phaser.Math.Between(5, 7) : Phaser.Math.Between(2, 3);
   126→    const rMax  = leg ? 2.2  : 1.6;
   127→    const aMax  = leg ? 0.90 : 0.70;
   128→    const riseMin = leg ? 20  : 14;
   129→    const riseMax = leg ? 42  : 28;
   130→    const lifeMin = leg ? 280 : 180;
   131→    const lifeMax = leg ? 520 : 380;
   132→
   133→    for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
   134→      const gfx = scene.add.graphics();
   135→      const r   = Phaser.Math.FloatBetween(0.8, rMax);
   136→      const col = Phaser.Utils.Array.GetRandom(cols) as number;
   137→      const a   = Phaser.Math.FloatBetween(0.50, aMax);
   138→
   139→      gfx.fillStyle(col, a);
   140→      gfx.fillCircle(0, 0, r);
   141→      gfx.setPosition(
   142→        px + Phaser.Math.Between(-10, 10),
   143→        py + Phaser.Math.Between(10, 24),
   144→      );
   145→      gfx.setDepth(88);
   146→
   147→      scene.tweens.add({
   148→        targets:  gfx,
   149→        y:        gfx.y - Phaser.Math.Between(riseMin, riseMax),
   150→        x:        gfx.x + Phaser.Math.Between(-4, 4),
   151→        alpha:    0,
   152→        scaleX:   0.15,
   153→        scaleY:   0.15,
   154→        duration: Phaser.Math.Between(lifeMin, lifeMax),
   155→        ease:     'Quad.easeOut',
   156→        onComplete: () => { gfx.destroy(); },
   157→      });
   158→    }
   159→  }
   160→
   161→  // ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
   162→  // B: 화염 추적선
   163→  // ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
   164→
   165→  private drawFireTrail(
   166→    scene: Phaser.Scene,
   167→    fromX: number, fromY: number,
   168→    toList: { x: number; y: number }[],
   169→  ): void {
   170→    if (toList.length === 0) return;
   171→
   172→    const gfx = scene.add.graphics();
   173→    gfx.setDepth(195);
   174→
   175→    for (const { x, y } of toList) {
   176→      gfx.lineStyle(3.5, 0xff6600, 0.50);
   177→      gfx.beginPath(); gfx.moveTo(fromX, fromY); gfx.lineTo(x, y); gfx.strokePath();
   178→      gfx.lineStyle(1.5, 0xffee00, 0.75);
   179→      gfx.beginPath(); gfx.moveTo(fromX, fromY); gfx.lineTo(x, y); gfx.strokePath();
   180→    }
   181→
   182→    scene.tweens.add({
   183→      targets: gfx, alpha: 0, duration: 200, ease: 'Quad.easeOut',
   184→      onComplete: () => { gfx.destroy(); },
   185→    });
   186→  }
   187→
   188→  // ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
   189→  // C: 레거시 모드 활성화
   190→  // ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
   191→
   192→  private activateLegacyMode(api: GameSceneAPI): void {
   193→    if (this.legacyModeActive) return;
   194→    this.legacyModeActive = true;
   195→    this.accum = 0;
   196→
   197→    const scene = api.scene;
   198→    const cx    = api.player.x;
   199→    const cy    = api.player.y;
   200→
   201→    // ─ 반투명 플래시 ──────────────────────────────────────────────
   202→    const flashOverlay = scene.add.graphics().setDepth(200);
   203→    flashOverlay.fillStyle(0xffee00, 1);
   204→    flashOverlay.fillRect(0, 0, 400, 600);
   205→    flashOverlay.setAlpha(0.38);
   206→    scene.tweens.add({
   207→      targets: flashOverlay, alpha: 0, duration: 220, ease: 'Quad.easeOut',
   208→      onComplete: () => { flashOverlay.destroy(); },
   209→    });
   210→
   211→    // ─ 팽창 링 2개 ───────────────────────────────────────────────
   212→    [{ delay: 0, maxScale: 4.3 }, { delay: 80, maxScale: 3.4 }].forEach(({ delay, maxScale }) => {
   213→      const ring = scene.add.graphics().setDepth(155);
   214→      ring.lineStyle(2.5, 0xffee00, 0.9);
   215→      ring.strokeEllipse(0, 0, 74, 98);
   216→      ring.setPosition(cx, cy);
   217→      scene.tweens.add({
   218→        targets: ring, scaleX: maxScale, scaleY: maxScale, alpha: 0,
   219→        duration: 500, delay, ease: 'Quad.easeOut',
   220→        onComplete: () => { ring.destroy(); },
   221→      });
   222→    });
   223→
   224→    // ─ 채움 타원 ─────────────────────────────────────────────────
   225→    const fill = scene.add.graphics().setDepth(154);
   226→    fill.fillStyle(0xffaa00, 0.28);
   227→    fill.fillEllipse(0, 0, 74, 98);
   228→    fill.setPosition(cx, cy);
   229→    scene.tweens.add({
   230→      targets: fill, alpha: 0, duration: 180, ease: 'Quad.easeOut',
   231→      onComplete: () => { fill.destroy(); },
   232→    });
   233→
   234→    // ─ 파편 8개 ──────────────────────────────────────────────────
   235→    for (let i = 0; i < 8; i++) {
   236→      const angle = (i / 8) * Math.PI * 2 + Phaser.Math.FloatBetween(-0.25, 0.25);
   237→      const speed = Phaser.Math.FloatBetween(40, 95);
   238→      const sw    = Phaser.Math.Between(5, 12);
   239→      const sh    = Phaser.Math.Between(2, 5);
   240→      const shard = scene.add.graphics().setDepth(156);
   241→      shard.fillStyle(0xffee00, 1);
   242→      shard.fillRect(-sw / 2, -sh / 2, sw, sh);
   243→      shard.setPosition(cx, cy).setRotation(angle);
   244→      scene.tweens.add({
   245→        targets: shard,
   246→        x: cx + Math.cos(angle) * speed, y: cy + Math.sin(angle) * speed,
   247→        alpha: 0, rotation: angle + Phaser.Math.FloatBetween(-1, 1),
   248→        duration: Phaser.Math.Between(270, 440), ease: 'Quad.easeOut',
   249→        onComplete: () => { shard.destroy(); },
   250→      });
   251→    }
   252→
   253→    // ─ 화면 전체 불꽃 폭발 (20개) ────────────────────────────────
   254→    const BURST_COLS = [0xff2200, 0xff6600, 0xffaa00, 0xffdd00, 0xffff88];
   255→    for (let i = 0; i < 20; i++) {
   256→      const gfx = scene.add.graphics();
   257→      const w   = Phaser.Math.Between(4, 10);
   258→      const h   = Phaser.Math.Between(10, 22);
   259→      const col = Phaser.Utils.Array.GetRandom(BURST_COLS) as number;
   260→
   261→      gfx.fillStyle(col, Phaser.Math.FloatBetween(0.22, 0.50));
   262→      gfx.fillPoints(LegacyAbility.flamePts(w, h), true);
   263→      gfx.fillStyle(0xffee88, Phaser.Math.FloatBetween(0.28, 0.55));
   264→      gfx.fillPoints(LegacyAbility.flamePts(w * 0.4, h * 0.5), true);
   265→
   266→      gfx.setPosition(Phaser.Math.Between(20, 380), Phaser.Math.Between(40, 560));
   267→      gfx.setRotation(Phaser.Math.DegToRad(Phaser.Math.Between(-180, 180)));
   268→      gfx.setDepth(158).setAlpha(0);
   269→
   270→      scene.tweens.add({
   271→        targets: gfx, alpha: 1,
   272→        scaleX: Phaser.Math.FloatBetween(1.2, 1.9),
   273→        scaleY: Phaser.Math.FloatBetween(1.2, 1.9),
   274→        duration: 140, delay: Phaser.Math.Between(0, 280), ease: 'Quad.easeOut',
   275→        onComplete: () => {
   276→          scene.tweens.add({
   277→            targets: gfx, alpha: 0, y: gfx.y - Phaser.Math.Between(20, 50),
   278→            duration: Phaser.Math.Between(380, 650), ease: 'Quad.easeIn',
   279→            onComplete: () => { gfx.destroy(); },
   280→          });
   281→        },
   282→      });
   283→    }
   284→
   285→    // ─ 상단 노란빛 그라데이션 ────────────────────────────────────
   286→    this.legacyTopGlow = scene.add.graphics();
   287→    this.legacyTopGlow.fillGradientStyle(0xffee00, 0xffee00, 0xffee00, 0xffee00, 0.28, 0.28, 0, 0);
   288→    this.legacyTopGlow.fillRect(0, 0, 400, 220);
   289→    this.legacyTopGlow.setDepth(148);
   290→
   291→    this.legacyPulseTween = scene.tweens.add({
   292→      targets: [this.legacyTopGlow], alpha: 0.55,
   293→      duration: 600, yoyo: true, repeat: -1, ease: 'Sine.easeInOut',
   294→    });

   296→    // ─ 레거시 모드 황금 빗줄기 (repeat: -1, 10초 후 remove로 정리) ─
   297→    this.legacyRainTimer = scene.time.addEvent({
   298→      delay: 120,
   299→      repeat: -1,
   300→      callback: () => { this.spawnRainStar(scene, LegacyAbility.RAIN_COLS_LEGACY, 0.75, 1.00, 3); },
   301→    });
   302→
   303→    // 레거시 모드 종료
   304→    this.legacyEndTimer = scene.time.delayedCall(LEGACY_PARAMS.legacyDuration, () => {
   305→      this.legacyModeActive = false;
   306→      this.legacyRainTimer?.remove();
   307→      this.legacyRainTimer = undefined;
   308→      this.legacyPulseTween?.stop();

   312→        onComplete: () => {
   313→          this.legacyTopGlow?.destroy();
   314→          this.legacyTopGlow = undefined;

   321→  // ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

   325→  private startFeverRain(api: GameSceneAPI): void {

   336→  /** 빗줄기 별 1개 생성 — startFeverRain / legacyRainTimer 공용 */
   337→  private spawnRainStar(
   338→    scene: Phaser.Scene,
   339→    cols: readonly number[],
   340→    aMin: number,
   341→    aMax: number,
   342→    count: number,
   343→  ): void {

   352→      gfx.fillCircle(0, 0, s * 0.32);
   353→
   354→      gfx.setPosition(Phaser.Math.Between(10, 390), -15);
   355→      gfx.setRotation(Phaser.Math.DegToRad(Phaser.Math.Between(0, 360)));
   356→      gfx.setDepth(85);
   357→
   358→      scene.tweens.add({
   359→        targets:  gfx,
   360→        y:        gfx.y + Phaser.Math.Between(450, 600),
   361→        x:        gfx.x + Phaser.Math.Between(-25, 25),
   362→        rotation: gfx.rotation + Phaser.Math.FloatBetween(1.5, 3.0),
   363→        alpha:    0,
   364→        duration: Phaser.Math.Between(2200, 3600),
   365→        ease:     'Linear',

   371→  // ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
   372→  // 불태우기 로직
   373→  // ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
   374→
   375→  private burnRandomPoops(api: GameSceneAPI, count: number): void {
   376→    const visible = api.poops.getChildren().filter(
   377→      c => c.active && (c as Phaser.Physics.Arcade.Sprite).y > -10,
   378→    ) as Phaser.Physics.Arcade.Sprite[];
   379→
   380→    if (visible.length === 0) return;
   381→
   382→    // Partial Fisher-Yates: count개만 앞으로 뽑아내고 나머지 순회 생략
   383→    const pool = visible.slice();
   384→    const take = Math.min(count, pool.length);
   385→    for (let i = 0; i < take; i++) {
   386→      const j = i + Math.floor(Math.random() * (pool.length - i));
   387→      [pool[i], pool[j]] = [pool[j], pool[i]];
   388→    }
   389→    const targets = pool.slice(0, take);
   390→
   391→    this.drawFireTrail(
   392→      api.scene,
   393→      api.player.x, api.player.y,
   394→      targets.map(p => ({ x: p.x, y: p.y })),
   395→    );
   396→
   397→    for (const poop of targets) {
   398→      if (!poop.active) continue;
   399→      this.playBurnEffect(api.scene, poop.x, poop.y);
   400→      poop.destroy();
   401→      api.updateScore(10);
   402→    }
   403→  }
   404→

   410→    this.startFeverEndTimer?.remove();
   411→    this.startFeverTimer?.remove();
   412→    this.legacyEndTimer?.remove();
   413→    this.legacyRainTimer?.remove();
   414→    this.legacyPulseTween?.stop();
   415→    this.legacyTopGlow?.destroy();
   416→  }

   421→
   422→  private playBurnEffect(scene: Phaser.Scene, cx: number, cy: number): void {
   423→    const flash = scene.add.graphics();
   424→    flash.fillStyle(0xff7700, 0.60); flash.fillCircle(0, 0, 17);
   425→    flash.fillStyle(0xffee88, 0.45); flash.fillCircle(0, 0, 9);
   426→    flash.setPosition(cx, cy).setDepth(202);
   427→    scene.tweens.add({
   428→      targets: flash, scaleX: 2.8, scaleY: 2.8, alpha: 0,
   429→      duration: 190, ease: 'Quad.easeOut',
   430→      onComplete: () => { flash.destroy(); },
   431→    });
   432→
   433→    for (let i = 0; i < 10; i++) {
   434→      const gfx = scene.add.graphics();
   435→      const w   = Phaser.Math.Between(5, 11);
   436→      const h   = Phaser.Math.Between(14, 27);
   437→
   438→      gfx.fillStyle(0xaa1100, 0.25); gfx.fillPoints(LegacyAbility.flamePts(w,        h       ), true);
   439→      gfx.fillStyle(0xff4400, 0.48); gfx.fillPoints(LegacyAbility.flamePts(w * 0.70, h * 0.76), true);
   440→      gfx.fillStyle(0xffcc00, 0.68); gfx.fillPoints(LegacyAbility.flamePts(w * 0.40, h * 0.50), true);
   441→
   442→      gfx.setPosition(
   443→        cx + Phaser.Math.Between(-15, 15),
   444→        cy + Phaser.Math.Between(-10, 10),
   445→      );
   446→      gfx.setRotation(Phaser.Math.DegToRad(Phaser.Math.Between(-28, 28)));
   447→      gfx.setDepth(200);
   448→
   449→      const rise = Phaser.Math.Between(48, 88);
   450→      const dur  = Phaser.Math.Between(500, 900);
   451→
   452→      scene.tweens.add({
   453→        targets: gfx, y: gfx.y - rise * 0.38, scaleX: 1.45, scaleY: 1.45,
   454→        duration: dur * 0.35, ease: 'Quad.easeOut',
   455→        onComplete: () => {
   456→          scene.tweens.add({
   457→            targets: gfx, y: gfx.y - rise * 0.62, scaleX: 0, scaleY: 0, alpha: 0,
   458→            duration: dur * 0.65, ease: 'Quad.easeIn',
   459→            onComplete: () => { gfx.destroy(); },
   460→          });
   461→        },
   462→      });
   463→    }
   464→
   465→    const EMBER_COLS = [0xff5500, 0xff8800, 0xffbb00];
   466→    for (let i = 0; i < 8; i++) {
   467→      const gfx = scene.add.graphics();
   468→      const s   = Phaser.Math.Between(2, 5);
   469→      const col = Phaser.Utils.Array.GetRandom(EMBER_COLS) as number;
   470→
   471→      gfx.fillStyle(col, 0.18);
   472→      gfx.fillPoints([{ x: 0, y: -s * 1.9 }, { x: s * 1.9, y: 0 }, { x: 0, y: s * 1.9 }, { x: -s * 1.9, y: 0 }], true);
   473→      gfx.fillStyle(0xffee88, 0.65);
   474→      gfx.fillPoints([{ x: 0, y: -s }, { x: s, y: 0 }, { x: 0, y: s }, { x: -s, y: 0 }], true);
   475→
   476→      gfx.setPosition(
   477→        cx + Phaser.Math.Between(-18, 18),
   478→        cy + Phaser.Math.Between(-18, 18),
   479→      ).setDepth(201);

   500→      gfx.fillCircle(0, 0, Phaser.Math.Between(6, 13));

     1→import Phaser from 'phaser';
     2→import { BaseAbility } from './BaseAbility';
     3→import type { GameSceneAPI } from './types';
     4→import { GLITCH_PARAMS } from '../config/abilityParams';
     5→
     6→/**
     7→ * 글리치 (SR) — 분신 소환
     8→ * ★0: 200점마다 1개  / ★1: 180점마다 1개
     9→ * ★2: 160점마다 2개  / ★3: 140점마다 2개 + 분신 소멸 시 주변 일반 똥 2개 파괴
    10→ */
    11→export class GlitchAbility extends BaseAbility {
    12→  private ghost?: Phaser.Physics.Arcade.Sprite;
    13→  private posHistory: Array<{ x: number; y: number }> = [];
    14→  private readonly TRAIL_FRAMES = 12; // ~0.2초 딜레이 (60fps 기준)
    15→  private attackTimers: Phaser.Time.TimerEvent[] = [];
    16→
    17→
    18→  override onCreate(api: GameSceneAPI): void {
    19→    // 초기 위치는 플레이어와 동일하게 (히스토리가 쌓이면서 자연스럽게 딜레이 생김)
    20→    this.ghost = api.scene.physics.add.sprite(api.player.x, api.player.y, 'glitch_front');
    21→    this.ghost.setDisplaySize(60, 80).setAlpha(0.45).setDepth(5);
    22→
    23→    // 중력 비활성화
    24→    const body = this.ghost.body as Phaser.Physics.Arcade.Body;
    25→    body.setAllowGravity(false);
    26→
    27→    // 일반 똥은 통과 (overlap 없음)
    28→    // 특수 똥은 분신이 닿으면 전체 수집 처리 (카운트++, 점수, 획득 텍스트)
    29→    const makeCollect = (
    30→      collectFn: (p: Phaser.Physics.Arcade.Sprite) => void,
    31→    ): Phaser.Types.Physics.Arcade.ArcadePhysicsCallback =>
    32→      (_, p) => {
    33→        const sp = p as Phaser.Physics.Arcade.Sprite;
    34→        if (!sp.active) return;
    35→        collectFn(sp);
    36→      };
    37→    api.scene.physics.add.overlap(this.ghost, api.goldPoops,    makeCollect(p => api.collectGoldPoop(p)));
    38→    api.scene.physics.add.overlap(this.ghost, api.diamondPoops, makeCollect(p => api.collectDiamondPoop(p)));
    39→    api.scene.physics.add.overlap(this.ghost, api.topazPoops,   makeCollect(p => api.collectTopazPoop(p)));
    40→    api.scene.physics.add.overlap(this.ghost, api.rainbowPoops, makeCollect(p => api.collectRainbowPoop(p)));
    41→  }
    42→
    43→  override onScoreMilestone(score: number, api: GameSceneAPI): void {
    44→    if (score % GLITCH_PARAMS.collectInterval === 0) {
    45→      this.collectSpecialWithGhost(api, GLITCH_PARAMS.collectCount);
    46→    }
    47→  }
    48→
    49→  override onUpdate(api: GameSceneAPI): void {
    50→    if (!this.ghost) return;
    51→    // 현재 플레이어 위치를 히스토리에 기록
    52→    this.posHistory.push({ x: api.player.x, y: api.player.y });
    53→    if (this.posHistory.length > this.TRAIL_FRAMES) {
    54→      // TRAIL_FRAMES 이전의 위치로 이동 → 약 0.2초 지연 잔상 효과
    55→      const target = this.posHistory.shift()!;
    56→      this.ghost.x = target.x;
    57→      this.ghost.y = target.y;
    58→    } else {
    59→      // 히스토리가 쌓이기 전까지는 플레이어 위치 유지
    60→      this.ghost.x = api.player.x;
    61→      this.ghost.y = api.player.y;
    62→    }
    63→  }
    64→
    65→  override onDestroy(_api: GameSceneAPI): void {
    66→    this.ghost?.destroy();
    67→    this.attackTimers.forEach(t => t.remove());
    68→  }
    69→
    70→  private collectSpecialWithGhost(api: GameSceneAPI, count: number): void {
    71→    if (!this.ghost) return;
    72→
    73→    const allSpecial = [
    74→      ...api.goldPoops.getChildren(),
    75→      ...api.diamondPoops.getChildren(),
    76→      ...api.topazPoops.getChildren(),
    77→      ...api.rainbowPoops.getChildren(),
    78→    ].filter(p => (p as Phaser.GameObjects.GameObject).active) as Phaser.Physics.Arcade.Sprite[];
    79→
    80→    if (allSpecial.length === 0) return;
    81→
    82→    const targets = allSpecial.slice(0, count);
    83→
    84→    targets.forEach(target => {
    85→      const attackGhost = api.scene.add
    86→        .image(target.x, target.y, 'glitch_front')
    87→        .setDisplaySize(60, 80)
    88→        .setAlpha(1.0)
    89→        .setDepth(6);
    90→
    91→      const timer = api.scene.time.delayedCall(1000, () => {
    92→        attackGhost.destroy();
    93→        if (!target.active) return;
    94→        if (api.goldPoops.contains(target))         api.collectGoldPoop(target);
    95→        else if (api.diamondPoops.contains(target)) api.collectDiamondPoop(target);
    96→        else if (api.topazPoops.contains(target))   api.collectTopazPoop(target);
    97→        else if (api.rainbowPoops.contains(target)) api.collectRainbowPoop(target);
    98→      });
    99→      this.attackTimers.push(timer);
   100→    });
   101→  }
   102→}
   103→

    10→export class HackerAbility extends BaseAbility {

    13→  override specialPoopSpeedReduction(_type: 'gold' | 'diamond'): number {

    21→    }

    24→  private removeRandomPoops(count: number, api: GameSceneAPI): void {
    25→    const active = api.poops
    26→      .getChildren()
    27→      .filter(p => (p as Phaser.GameObjects.GameObject).active) as Phaser.Physics.Arcade.Sprite[];
    28→
    29→    active.sort(() => Math.random() - 0.5);
    30→    active.slice(0, count).forEach(p => {
    31→      (p.body as Phaser.Physics.Arcade.Body).setVelocity(0, 0);
    32→      this.playTerminalEffect(p.x, p.y, api);
    33→      p.destroy();
    34→    });
    35→  }
    36→
    37→  /** 초록 테두리 검정 박스 flash → 픽셀 비산 */
    38→  private playTerminalEffect(cx: number, cy: number, api: GameSceneAPI): void {
    39→    const scene = api.scene;
    40→    const BOX_W = 36, BOX_H = 36;
    41→
    42→    const box = scene.add.graphics().setDepth(110);
    43→    box.fillStyle(0x000000, 1);
    44→    box.fillRect(-BOX_W / 2, -BOX_H / 2, BOX_W, BOX_H);
    45→    box.lineStyle(2, 0x00ff41, 1);
    46→    box.strokeRect(-BOX_W / 2, -BOX_H / 2, BOX_W, BOX_H);
    47→    box.setPosition(cx, cy);
    48→
    49→    scene.tweens.add({
    50→      targets: box,
    51→      alpha: 0,
    52→      duration: 180,
    53→      delay: 60,
    54→      onComplete: () => box.destroy(),
    55→    });
    56→
    57→    const PIXEL_COUNT = 10;
    58→    const GREEN = 0x00ff41;
    59→
    60→    for (let i = 0; i < PIXEL_COUNT; i++) {
    61→      const size  = Phaser.Math.Between(4, 9);
    62→      const angle = Phaser.Math.FloatBetween(0, Math.PI * 2);
    63→      const speed = Phaser.Math.FloatBetween(40, 110);
    64→      const tx    = cx + Math.cos(angle) * speed;
    65→      const ty    = cy + Math.sin(angle) * speed;
    66→
    67→      const px = scene.add.graphics().setDepth(111);
    68→      px.fillStyle(GREEN, 1);
    69→      px.fillRect(0, 0, size, size);
    70→      px.setPosition(cx, cy);
    71→
    72→      scene.tweens.add({

    75→        y: ty + Phaser.Math.Between(10, 30),
    76→        alpha: 0,
    77→        duration: Phaser.Math.Between(250, 420),
    78→        ease: 'Quad.easeOut',
    79→        onComplete: () => px.destroy(),
    80→      });
    81→    }
    82→  }
    83→}

이제 전체 코드를 분석 완료했습니다. 핵심 원인들을 정리합니다.

`★ Insight ─────────────────────────────────────`
**가장 큰 원인: 풀링된 오브젝트를 `destroy()`로 제거**
- Object Pool 패턴을 사용하면서도 수집/제거 시 `destroy()`를 호출 → 풀에서 완전히 제거됨
- 이후 `group.get()`은 새 인스턴스를 매번 생성해야 함 → GC 압박 → 프레임 드롭
- 올바른 방법: `setActive(false).setVisible(false) + body.setEnable(false)` → 풀에 반환

**두 번째 원인: GlitchAbility 타이머 배열 무한 증가**
- 완료된 타이머가 배열에서 제거되지 않음

**세 번째 원인: LegacyAbility 이펙트 과다**
- 불꽃 오라 + 빗줄기 + 소멸 이펙트가 동시에 수십~백 개의 Graphics + Tween 생성
`─────────────────────────────────────────────────`

수정을 시작합니다.

> AGENT

성능 최적화 작업이 완료되었습니다. 7개 파일에 걸쳐 적용된 수정 사항을 정리하면:

`★ Insight ─────────────────────────────────────`
**게임 버벅임의 주요 원인 3가지:**
1. **오브젝트 풀 파괴**: `destroy()`로 풀에서 완전 제거 → 새 객체 계속 생성 → GC 압박
2. **과도한 파티클**: LegacyAbility 불꽃/연기 이펙트가 한 번에 수십 개 Graphics+Tween 생성
3. **타이머 배열 누적**: GlitchAbility의 완료된 타이머가 배열에 계속 남아 메모리 증가
`─────────────────────────────────────────────────`

### 적용된 수정:

| 파일 | 수정 내용 |
|------|----------|
| `Poop.ts` / `PoolablePoopBase.ts` | `recycle()` 메서드 추가 — destroy 대신 비활성화로 풀 재사용 |
| `GameScene.ts` | 특수똥 수집·센티넬 흡수 시 `destroy()` → `recycle()` |
| `SentinelAbility.ts` | `zapNearbyPoops`에서 destroy → 인라인 비활성화 |
| `LegacyAbility.ts` | destroy → 비활성화 + 파티클 수 감소 (불꽃 10→6, 잔불 8→5, 연기 4→2, 버스트 20→12) |
| `GlitchAbility.ts` | 완료된 타이머 자기제거 (배열 누적 방지) |
| `HackerAbility.ts` | 픽셀 파편 10→6개 |

타입 체크 통과 완료입니다. 테스트 후 커밋/푸시할까요, 아니면 바로 커밋해도 될까요?

## Findings

### 1. DUPLICATE: `Poop.recycle()` is identical to `PoolablePoopBase.recycle()` (HIGH priority)

`Poop` (`/Users/johyunduk/Projects/ddong-avoid-game/src/objects/Poop.ts`, line 83-90) defines a `recycle()` method that is **character-for-character identical** to `PoolablePoopBase.recycle()` (`/Users/johyunduk/Projects/ddong-avoid-game/src/objects/PoolablePoopBase.ts`, line 23-30):

```typescript
recycle() {
    this.setActive(false).setVisible(false);
    const body = this.body as Phaser.Physics.Arcade.Body;
    if (body) {
      body.setVelocity(0, 0);
      body.setEnable(false);
    }
}
```

The root cause: **`Poop` extends `Phaser.Physics.Arcade.Sprite` directly** (line 14) instead of extending `PoolablePoopBase`. Meanwhile, `GoldPoop`, `DiamondPoop`, `TopazPoop`, and `RainbowPoop` all extend `PoolablePoopBase` and inherit `recycle()` for free. `Poop` is the only object class that duplicates the method instead of inheriting it.

The `update()` method is also duplicated -- `Poop.update()` (line 92-96) matches `PoolablePoopBase.update()` (line 32-36) in logic.

**Fix**: Make `Poop` extend `PoolablePoopBase` and remove the duplicate `recycle()` and `update()`. Note that `Poop` has a more complex `reinit()` (texture randomization, difficulty-based sizing), so only `recycle()` and `update()` would be inherited as-is.

### 2. DUPLICATE: Inline deactivation in `LegacyAbility` and `SentinelAbility` should call `recycle()` (MEDIUM priority)

Two ability files inline the exact same deactivation pattern instead of calling the existing `recycle()` method:

- **`LegacyAbility.ts`** line 401-403 (`burnRandomPoops`): Iterates over `api.poops` children and does `poop.setActive(false).setVisible(false)` + body disable inline.
- **`SentinelAbility.ts`** line 210-212 (`zapNearbyPoops`): Same inline pattern on `api.poops` children.

Both operate on sprites from `api.poops`, which contains `Poop` instances -- and `Poop` now has `recycle()`. The diff already uses `(poop as unknown as { recycle(): void }).recycle()` in `GameScene.ts`. The same cast pattern could replace the inline code in both abilities.

There are also two additional occurrences in `GameScene.ts` itself:

- Line 327-329 (`prewarm` lambda): Inlines the deactivation pattern on freshly created pool objects. This is a constructor/init context where `recycle()` would semantically work, but the objects are brand new and already inactive from their constructors, so this is arguably a different use case (prewarm safety net).
- Line 910-912 (fever time conversion): Inlines the pattern on `Poop` sprites. This could call `poopSprite.recycle()` directly since `Poop` has the method (and TypeScript would know about it since `poopSprite` is already typed as `Poop`).

### 3. OBSERVATION: The `as unknown as { recycle(): void }` cast is a code smell

`GameScene.ts` uses `(poop as unknown as { recycle(): void }).recycle()` in two places (lines 754, 1145). This double cast through `unknown` is fragile -- there is no compile-time guarantee that `recycle()` exists. If `Poop` extended `PoolablePoopBase`, or if both `Poop` and `PoolablePoopBase` implemented a shared interface (e.g., `Recyclable`), the cast would be unnecessary and type-safe.

### 4. NO ISSUE: GlitchAbility timer cleanup

The added timer self-removal in `GlitchAbility.ts` (line 92-94) is unique to that class. No shared utility exists or is needed for this pattern -- it is a straightforward array splice on `this.attackTimers`. No duplication concern.

### 5. NO ISSUE: Particle count reductions

The particle count reductions in `HackerAbility` (10 to 6) and `LegacyAbility` (20 to 12, 10 to 6, 8 to 5, 4 to 2) are simple constant changes for performance tuning. No code reuse concern.

| MEDIUM | Inline deactivation in `LegacyAbility.burnRandomPoops` | `src/abilities/LegacyAbility.ts:401-403` | Cast to `{ recycle(): void }` and call `recycle()` |

## Code Quality Review Findings

### 1. Copy-paste: `recycle()` duplicated in `Poop.ts` and `PoolablePoopBase.ts`

**Finding: Confirmed -- identical method on two unrelated classes.**

- `Poop` (`src/objects/Poop.ts:14`) extends `Phaser.Physics.Arcade.Sprite` directly.
- `PoolablePoopBase` (`src/objects/PoolablePoopBase.ts:11`) also extends `Phaser.Physics.Arcade.Sprite` directly.
- `GoldPoop`, `DiamondPoop`, `TopazPoop`, `RainbowPoop` all extend `PoolablePoopBase` and inherit its `recycle()`.
- `Poop` does **not** extend `PoolablePoopBase`. It has its own copy-pasted `recycle()` (lines 83-90) that is byte-for-byte identical to `PoolablePoopBase.recycle()` (lines 23-30). The `update()` method is also duplicated identically between them.

**Why this matters:** `Poop` should extend `PoolablePoopBase` instead of `Phaser.Physics.Arcade.Sprite`. It already follows the exact same pool pattern (inactive-by-default constructor, `reinit()` to activate, `recycle()` to deactivate, `update()` to auto-recycle). The only reason it doesn't seems to be that `Poop` was written first and `PoolablePoopBase` was extracted later for the special poop types, but nobody went back to make `Poop` use it. `Poop.reinit()` has a more complex signature (takes `difficulty`), but that's fine -- it would just override the base `reinit()`.

### 2. Copy-paste: Inline deactivation in `LegacyAbility.ts` and `SentinelAbility.ts`

**Finding: Confirmed -- the same 3-line deactivation pattern appears in 3 places that should call `recycle()`.**

The inline pattern at:
- `LegacyAbility.ts:401-403` (`burnRandomPoops`)
- `SentinelAbility.ts:210-212` (`zapNearbyPoops`)

is identical to `PoolablePoopBase.recycle()` / `Poop.recycle()`. The abilities operate on `Phaser.Physics.Arcade.Sprite` references obtained from `api.poops.getChildren()`, which are actually `Poop` instances. Both `Poop` and `PoolablePoopBase` subclasses have `recycle()`, so the ability code could call it directly.

However, the abilities face the same type problem as GameScene: the `api.poops` group is typed as `Phaser.Physics.Arcade.Group`, whose `getChildren()` returns `Phaser.GameObjects.GameObject[]`. There is no shared interface or type that exposes `recycle()`. So the inline copy-paste is a symptom of **finding #3** below.

### 3. Leaky abstraction / unsafe cast: `(poop as unknown as { recycle(): void }).recycle()`

**Finding: Confirmed -- this is the central design gap causing all the duplication.**

The double cast through `unknown` appears at:
- `GameScene.ts:754` in `handleSpecialCollected` (parameter is `Phaser.Physics.Arcade.Sprite`)
- `GameScene.ts:1145` in `hitPoop` (parameter is `Phaser.Types.Physics.Arcade.GameObjectWithBody | Phaser.Tilemaps.Tile`)

The root cause: there is no shared `Recyclable` interface that `Poop` and `PoolablePoopBase` both implement. Since all poop objects in the game (both `Poop` and the `PoolablePoopBase` subclasses) have `recycle()`, the fix is straightforward:

1. Define a `Recyclable` interface (e.g., `{ recycle(): void }`) or add `recycle()` to an existing shared type.
2. Make both `Poop` and `PoolablePoopBase` implement it (they already do at runtime).
3. Use that interface in `GameSceneAPI` (e.g., type the group children), or create a helper function that casts once in a central location.

This would eliminate:
- Both `as unknown as { recycle(): void }` casts in GameScene
- The inline deactivation copy-paste in LegacyAbility and SentinelAbility (they could cast to `Recyclable` and call `recycle()`)

### 4. GlitchAbility timer self-cleanup

**Finding: The implementation is correct; no redundant state issue.**

The self-cleanup pattern (lines 93-95) properly removes completed timers from `this.attackTimers`. This is sound because:

- Each timer fires at most once (it's a `delayedCall`, not a repeating timer).
- On fire, it removes itself from the array via `indexOf` + `splice`.
- On game over, `onDestroy()` (line 67) calls `.remove()` on any timers that haven't fired yet.
- After the self-cleanup, the `onDestroy` loop becomes a no-op for already-removed timers, which is harmless.

The only minor observation is that `indexOf` on an array of timer references is O(n) per removal, but since `GLITCH_PARAMS.collectCount` is at most 2 and the timers fire after 1 second, the array will realistically never exceed ~4 entries. This is not a problem.

### Summary of Actionable Issues

| Priority | Issue | Location | Fix |
|----------|-------|----------|-----|
| **High** | Missing shared `Recyclable` interface causes `as unknown as` double casts and inline duplication | `GameScene.ts`, `LegacyAbility.ts`, `SentinelAbility.ts` | Define interface, implement on both poop base classes |
| **Medium** | `Poop` duplicates `recycle()` and `update()` from `PoolablePoopBase` instead of extending it | `Poop.ts` vs `PoolablePoopBase.ts` | Make `Poop` extend `PoolablePoopBase` |
| **Low** | Inline deactivation in abilities is copy-paste of `recycle()` | `LegacyAbility.ts:401-403`, `SentinelAbility.ts:210-212` | Call `recycle()` via the shared interface after fix #1 |
| **None** | GlitchAbility timer self-cleanup | `GlitchAbility.ts:93-95` | Correct as-is |

## Efficiency Review Findings

### 1. Memory: Poop group `maxSize` -- GOOD

All groups have `maxSize` set in `GameScene.ts` (lines 288-319):
- `poops`: maxSize 60
- `goldPoops`: maxSize 20
- `diamondPoops`: maxSize 20
- `topazPoops`: maxSize 10
- `rainbowPoops`: maxSize 10

With `maxSize`, Phaser's `Group.get()` returns `null` when the pool is exhausted (and the code already checks `if (!poop) continue`). Recycled sprites are reused via `group.get()` which first looks for inactive members. **No unbounded growth concern here.**

### 2. Memory: SentinelAbility `zapNearbyPoops` using `setActive(false)` without `destroy()` -- ACCEPTABLE BUT MISMATCH

SentinelAbility (line 209-212) sets poops to `setActive(false).setVisible(false)` and disables the body. These sprites remain in the `poops` group, which is the intended pool behavior -- they will be reused by `this.poops.get()` on the next spawn. Since `maxSize` is 60, the pool is bounded. **No leak.**

However, note that `zapNearbyPoops` does NOT call `poop.recycle()` -- it manually inlines the same logic. This is a minor code duplication issue (not a functional bug), since the operations are identical.

### 3. Hot path: Does `body.setEnable(false)` stop physics processing? -- YES, WITH A CAVEAT

In Phaser 3's Arcade Physics, `body.setEnable(false)` sets `body.enable = false`. The `World.step()` method skips disabled bodies during its `postUpdate` loop -- bodies with `enable === false` are not processed for velocity, acceleration, overlap checks, etc. **This is correct and efficient.**

However, in the `update()` method of `Poop.ts` (line 92-96) and `PoolablePoopBase.ts` (line 32-36), the `update()` runs because `runChildUpdate: true` is set on the group. Phaser calls `update()` on ALL children in the group, not just active ones. Since `Poop.update()` checks `this.y > ...` on recycled (inactive) sprites, this means:

**Finding: Every recycled sprite in the pool still has its `update()` called every frame.** For the `poops` group with up to 60 members, this means up to ~60 no-op `update()` calls per frame. Each call reads `this.y` and `this.scene.cameras.main.height` before bailing because `this.y` is not > height+offset (recycled sprites have y set by `body.reset()` to wherever they were last placed, or their position is frozen).

**Recommendation:** Add `if (!this.active) return;` as the first line of `update()` in both `Poop.ts` and `PoolablePoopBase.ts` to skip recycled sprites early. This is a minor hot-path optimization (~60 property reads saved per frame).

### 4. Abilities still using `destroy()` on pool objects -- BUG

Two abilities call `.destroy()` on poop sprites that belong to the pooled `poops` group:

- **HackerAbility.ts line 33**: `p.destroy()` -- destroys poop sprites from the pool. When `destroy()` is called on a group member, Phaser removes it from the group entirely. This means the pool shrinks permanently. Over time, if HackerAbility fires many times, the effective pool size decreases, forcing Phaser to create new instances (up to `maxSize`). Worse, destroyed sprites can never be recycled back -- the pool gradually degrades.

- **MaehwaAbility.ts line 46**: `poop.destroy()` -- same problem. Each slash permanently removes a poop from the pool.

**This is the most significant finding.** Both should be changed to `poop.recycle()` (or the manual equivalent used in LegacyAbility/SentinelAbility) to preserve pool integrity.

### 5. Unbounded growth: GlitchAbility attackTimers -- ADEQUATE

The `attackTimers` array (line 15) grows by 1-2 entries per milestone trigger (via `collectSpecialWithGhost`). Each timer fires after 1000ms, at which point the callback removes itself from the array (lines 93-95). The array is also cleaned up in `onDestroy` (line 67).

**Worst case:** If milestones fire very rapidly (e.g., every few seconds), the array might briefly hold 2-4 entries. Since each entry self-cleans after 1 second, and milestones happen every 140-200 points (which takes many seconds), **the array is effectively bounded at a small number (typically 0-4 entries). No concern.**

### 6. Particle count reductions -- REASONABLE

The diff mentions reducing particles in LegacyAbility:
- Burst: 20 -> 12 (line 255)
- Flames: 10 -> 6 (line 436)
- Embers: 8 -> 5 (line 469)
- Smoke: 4 -> 2 (line 500)

Looking at the current code, these counts are already at the reduced values. Each effect creates short-lived Graphics objects with tweens (180-1350ms lifetime). At these counts:
- `playBurnEffect` creates: 1 flash + 6 flames + 5 embers + 2 smoke = **14 Graphics objects per burn**
- Multiple burns can fire per spawn cycle (`burnRandomPoops` removes up to 2-4 poops at once)
- Worst case burst: 4 poops x 14 = 56 temporary Graphics objects

**These counts are reasonable for a 400x600 canvas.** The visual density should still be adequate. The main concern is that each Graphics object + tween is individually created and destroyed, but given the short lifetimes and small counts, this should not cause frame drops on mobile.

### Additional finding: LegacyAbility `spawnOraFlame` -- potential hot-path concern

`spawnOraFlame` (line 119) creates 2-7 Graphics objects every 80-180ms (depending on legacy mode). In legacy mode, that's up to 7 objects every 80ms = ~87 objects/second, each living 280-520ms. At steady state, there could be **~25-45 simultaneous Graphics objects** just from the ora flame effect alone, plus rain stars (3 per 120ms in legacy mode = ~75 objects at 2200-3600ms lifetime).

This is the heaviest per-frame allocation in the codebase. It is not part of the diff but worth noting as a potential performance bottleneck on lower-end devices.

---

## Summary

| # | Concern | Severity | Status |
|---|---------|----------|--------|
| 1 | Pool `maxSize` bounds | -- | Good, all groups bounded |
| 2 | SentinelAbility pool sprites | Low | No leak; minor code duplication with `recycle()` |
| 3 | `setEnable(false)` physics skip | Low | Works correctly; but `update()` still called on inactive sprites (add early-return guard) |
| 4 | HackerAbility + MaehwaAbility `destroy()` | **High** | Permanently removes sprites from the pool, defeating the pooling system |
| 5 | GlitchAbility attackTimers growth | -- | Self-cleaning, effectively bounded |
| 6 | Particle count reductions | -- | Reasonable for visual quality |

**The critical issue is #4**: `HackerAbility.ts:33` and `MaehwaAbility.ts:46` call `.destroy()` on pooled poop sprites. This silently degrades the pool over the course of a game session. Both should use `.recycle()` instead.

     1→import Phaser from 'phaser';
     2→import { POOP_CONFIG } from '../config/poop';
     3→
     4→/**
     5→ * Object Pool 지원 똥 오브젝트의 공통 베이스 클래스
     6→ * GoldPoop, DiamondPoop, TopazPoop, RainbowPoop에서 상속
     7→ *
     8→ * - reinit(x, y): 풀에서 꺼낼 때 위치·활성화 재설정 (속도는 호출자가 설정)
     9→ * - update(): 화면 밖으로 나가면 destroy 대신 비활성화하여 풀에 반환
    10→ */
    11→export default class PoolablePoopBase extends Phaser.Physics.Arcade.Sprite {
    12→  reinit(x: number, y: number) {
    13→    this.setActive(true).setVisible(true);
    14→    const body = this.body as Phaser.Physics.Arcade.Body;
    15→    if (body) {
    16→      body.reset(x, y); // position + velocity 초기화 (내부에서 setPosition 호출)
    17→      body.setEnable(true);
    18→    }
    19→    // 속도는 호출자(GameScene)가 설정
    20→  }
    21→
    22→  /** 풀에 반환 (destroy 대신 비활성화) */
    23→  recycle() {
    24→    this.setActive(false).setVisible(false);
    25→    const body = this.body as Phaser.Physics.Arcade.Body;
    26→    if (body) {
    27→      body.setVelocity(0, 0);
    28→      body.setEnable(false);
    29→    }
    30→  }
    31→
    32→  update() {
    33→    if (this.y > this.scene.cameras.main.height + POOP_CONFIG.destroyOffset) {
    34→      this.recycle();
    35→    }
    36→  }
    37→}
    38→

     1→import Phaser from 'phaser';
     2→import { BaseAbility } from './BaseAbility';
     3→import type { GameSceneAPI } from './types';
     4→import { HACKER_PARAMS } from '../config/abilityParams';
     5→
     6→/**
     7→ * 루트 (SR) — 터미널 삭제
     8→ * 수치: HACKER_PARAMS 참조
     9→ */
    10→export class HackerAbility extends BaseAbility {
    11→  private lastHackerScore = 0;
    12→
    13→  override specialPoopSpeedReduction(_type: 'gold' | 'diamond'): number {
    14→    return HACKER_PARAMS.specialPoopSlowdown;
    15→  }
    16→
    17→  override onScoreMilestone(score: number, api: GameSceneAPI): void {
    18→    if (score % HACKER_PARAMS.deleteInterval === 0 && score > this.lastHackerScore) {
    19→      this.lastHackerScore = score;
    20→      this.removeRandomPoops(HACKER_PARAMS.deleteCount, api);
    21→    }
    22→  }
    23→
    24→  private removeRandomPoops(count: number, api: GameSceneAPI): void {
    25→    const active = api.poops
    26→      .getChildren()
    27→      .filter(p => (p as Phaser.GameObjects.GameObject).active) as Phaser.Physics.Arcade.Sprite[];
    28→
    29→    active.sort(() => Math.random() - 0.5);
    30→    active.slice(0, count).forEach(p => {
    31→      (p.body as Phaser.Physics.Arcade.Body).setVelocity(0, 0);
    32→      this.playTerminalEffect(p.x, p.y, api);
    33→      p.destroy();
    34→    });
    35→  }
    36→
    37→  /** 초록 테두리 검정 박스 flash → 픽셀 비산 */
    38→  private playTerminalEffect(cx: number, cy: number, api: GameSceneAPI): void {
    39→    const scene = api.scene;
    40→    const BOX_W = 36, BOX_H = 36;
    41→
    42→    const box = scene.add.graphics().setDepth(110);
    43→    box.fillStyle(0x000000, 1);
    44→    box.fillRect(-BOX_W / 2, -BOX_H / 2, BOX_W, BOX_H);
    45→    box.lineStyle(2, 0x00ff41, 1);
    46→    box.strokeRect(-BOX_W / 2, -BOX_H / 2, BOX_W, BOX_H);
    47→    box.setPosition(cx, cy);
    48→
    49→    scene.tweens.add({
    50→      targets: box,
    51→      alpha: 0,
    52→      duration: 180,
    53→      delay: 60,
    54→      onComplete: () => box.destroy(),
    55→    });
    56→
    57→    const PIXEL_COUNT = 6;
    58→    const GREEN = 0x00ff41;
    59→
    60→    for (let i = 0; i < PIXEL_COUNT; i++) {
    61→      const size  = Phaser.Math.Between(4, 9);
    62→      const angle = Phaser.Math.FloatBetween(0, Math.PI * 2);
    63→      const speed = Phaser.Math.FloatBetween(40, 110);
    64→      const tx    = cx + Math.cos(angle) * speed;
    65→      const ty    = cy + Math.sin(angle) * speed;
    66→
    67→      const px = scene.add.graphics().setDepth(111);
    68→      px.fillStyle(GREEN, 1);
    69→      px.fillRect(0, 0, size, size);
    70→      px.setPosition(cx, cy);
    71→
    72→      scene.tweens.add({
    73→        targets: px,
    74→        x: tx,
    75→        y: ty + Phaser.Math.Between(10, 30),
    76→        alpha: 0,
    77→        duration: Phaser.Math.Between(250, 420),
    78→        ease: 'Quad.easeOut',
    79→        onComplete: () => px.destroy(),
    80→      });
    81→    }
    82→  }
    83→}
    84→

     1→import { BaseAbility } from './BaseAbility';
     2→import type { GameSceneAPI } from './types';
     3→import { LEGACY_PARAMS } from '../config/abilityParams';
     4→
     5→/**
     6→ * 레거시 (UR) — 피버 + 레거시 모드
     7→ * ★0: 6초 피버 / 불태우기 30%/2개(일반) 60%/4개(레거시) / 500점마다 / 10초 / 1.2배
     8→ * ★1: 8초 / 35%/2개 65%/4개 / 450점마다 / 10초 / 1.25배
     9→ * ★2: 10초 / 40%/3개 70%/4개 / 400점마다 / 12초 / 1.30배
    10→ * ★3: 12초 / 45%/3개 75%/4개 / 350점마다 / 15초 / 1.35배 + 모드 종료 직후 3초 미니 피버
    11→ */
    12→export class LegacyAbility extends BaseAbility {
    13→  private startFeverActive = false;
    14→  private legacyModeActive = false;
    15→  private accum           = 0;
    16→  private lastLegacyScore = 0;
    17→
    18→  private legacyTopGlow?:       Phaser.GameObjects.Graphics;
    19→  private legacyPulseTween?:    Phaser.Tweens.Tween;
    20→  private legacyRainTimer?:     Phaser.Time.TimerEvent;
    21→  private legacyEndTimer?:      Phaser.Time.TimerEvent; // activateLegacyMode 종료 delayedCall
    22→  private startFeverTimer?:     Phaser.Time.TimerEvent; // startFeverRain addEvent
    23→  private startFeverEndTimer?:  Phaser.Time.TimerEvent; // onCreate 피버 종료 delayedCall
    24→
    25→  // A: 상시 불꽃 오라
    26→  private oraTimer = 0;
    27→  private static readonly ORA_INTERVAL_NORMAL = 180; // ms
    28→  private static readonly ORA_INTERVAL_LEGACY =  80; // ms
    29→  private static readonly ORA_COLS_NORMAL = [0xff3300, 0xff6600, 0xffaa00];
    30→  private static readonly ORA_COLS_LEGACY = [0xffaa00, 0xffcc00, 0xffee44, 0xffffff];
    31→
    32→  // D: 빗줄기 색상
    33→  private static readonly RAIN_COLS_FEVER  = [0xff8800, 0xffaa00, 0xffcc00, 0xffee00];
    34→  private static readonly RAIN_COLS_LEGACY = [0xff5500, 0xff8800, 0xffaa00, 0xffcc00];
    35→
    36→  // ── 불꽃 물방울 다각형 좌표 ─────────────────────────────────────────
    37→  private static flamePts(w: number, h: number) {
    38→    return [
    39→      { x:  0,         y: -h        },
    40→      { x:  w * 0.55,  y: -h * 0.1  },
    41→      { x:  w * 0.75,  y:  h * 0.35 },
    42→      { x:  w * 0.3,   y:  h        },
    43→      { x: -w * 0.3,   y:  h        },
    44→      { x: -w * 0.75,  y:  h * 0.35 },
    45→      { x: -w * 0.55,  y: -h * 0.1  },
    46→    ];
    47→  }
    48→
    49→  // ── 8각 별 좌표 (빗줄기 공용) ────────────────────────────────────────
    50→  private static starPts(s: number) {
    51→    return [
    52→      { x:  0,       y: -s       }, { x:  s * 0.4, y: -s * 0.4 },
    53→      { x:  s,       y:  0       }, { x:  s * 0.4, y:  s * 0.4 },
    54→      { x:  0,       y:  s       }, { x: -s * 0.4, y:  s * 0.4 },
    55→      { x: -s,       y:  0       }, { x: -s * 0.4, y: -s * 0.4 },
    56→    ];
    57→  }
    58→
    59→  // ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    60→  // 생명주기
    61→  // ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    62→
    63→  override onCreate(api: GameSceneAPI): void {
    64→    this.startFeverActive = true;
    65→    this.startFeverRain(api);
    66→    this.startFeverEndTimer = api.scene.time.delayedCall(LEGACY_PARAMS.feverDuration, () => {
    67→      this.startFeverActive = false;
    68→    });
    69→  }
    70→
    71→  override onUpdate(api: GameSceneAPI): void {
    72→    const delta = api.scene.game.loop.delta;
    73→    this.oraTimer += delta;
    74→    const interval = this.legacyModeActive
    75→      ? LegacyAbility.ORA_INTERVAL_LEGACY
    76→      : LegacyAbility.ORA_INTERVAL_NORMAL;
    77→    if (this.oraTimer >= interval) {
    78→      this.oraTimer -= interval;
    79→      this.spawnOraFlame(api);
    80→    }
    81→  }
    82→
    83→  override getTickScore(base: number): number {
    84→    if (!this.legacyModeActive) return base;
    85→    this.accum += base * LEGACY_PARAMS.scoreExtra;
    86→    const bonus = Math.floor(this.accum);
    87→    this.accum -= bonus;
    88→    return base + bonus;
    89→  }
    90→
    91→  override overrideSpawnPoop(api: GameSceneAPI): boolean {
    92→    if (this.startFeverActive) {
    93→      api.spawnGoldPoop();
    94→      api.spawnGoldPoop();
    95→      return true;
    96→    }
    97→    return false;
    98→  }
    99→
   100→  override onAfterSpawnPoop(api: GameSceneAPI): void {
   101→    const chance = this.legacyModeActive ? LEGACY_PARAMS.burnChanceLegacy : LEGACY_PARAMS.burnChanceNormal;
   102→    const count  = this.legacyModeActive ? LEGACY_PARAMS.burnCountLegacy  : LEGACY_PARAMS.burnCountNormal;
   103→    if (Math.random() < chance) {

   110→      this.lastLegacyScore = score;
   111→      this.activateLegacyMode(api);

   118→
   119→  private spawnOraFlame(api: GameSceneAPI): void {
   120→    const scene = api.scene;
   121→    const px    = api.player.x;
   122→    const py    = api.player.y;
   123→    const leg   = this.legacyModeActive;

   129→    const riseMax = leg ? 42  : 28;

   131→    const lifeMax = leg ? 520 : 380;

   133→    for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {

   144→      );
   145→      gfx.setDepth(88);

   147→      scene.tweens.add({

   151→        alpha:    0,

   154→        duration: Phaser.Math.Between(lifeMin, lifeMax),
   155→        ease:     'Quad.easeOut',
   156→        onComplete: () => { gfx.destroy(); },

   159→  }
   160→
   161→  // ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
   162→  // B: 화염 추적선
   163→  // ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
   164→
   165→  private drawFireTrail(
   166→    scene: Phaser.Scene,
   167→    fromX: number, fromY: number,
   168→    toList: { x: number; y: number }[],

   170→    if (toList.length === 0) return;
   171→
   172→    const gfx = scene.add.graphics();

   182→    scene.tweens.add({

   186→  }
   187→
   188→  // ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
   189→  // C: 레거시 모드 활성화

   191→
   192→  private activateLegacyMode(api: GameSceneAPI): void {
   193→    if (this.legacyModeActive) return;
   194→    this.legacyModeActive = true;
   195→    this.accum = 0;
   196→
   197→    const scene = api.scene;

   210→
   211→    // ─ 팽창 링 2개 ───────────────────────────────────────────────
   212→    [{ delay: 0, maxScale: 4.3 }, { delay: 80, maxScale: 3.4 }].forEach(({ delay, maxScale }) => {
   213→      const ring = scene.add.graphics().setDepth(155);
   214→      ring.lineStyle(2.5, 0xffee00, 0.9);

   229→    scene.tweens.add({

   231→      onComplete: () => { fill.destroy(); },
   232→    });
   233→
   234→    // ─ 파편 8개 ──────────────────────────────────────────────────
   235→    for (let i = 0; i < 8; i++) {
   236→      const angle = (i / 8) * Math.PI * 2 + Phaser.Math.FloatBetween(-0.25, 0.25);
   237→      const speed = Phaser.Math.FloatBetween(40, 95);

   248→        duration: Phaser.Math.Between(270, 440), ease: 'Quad.easeOut',
   249→        onComplete: () => { shard.destroy(); },

   251→    }

   253→    // ─ 화면 전체 불꽃 폭발 ────────────────────────────────

   255→    for (let i = 0; i < 12; i++) {

   266→      gfx.setPosition(Phaser.Math.Between(20, 380), Phaser.Math.Between(40, 560));
   267→      gfx.setRotation(Phaser.Math.DegToRad(Phaser.Math.Between(-180, 180)));

   281→        },

   283→    }
   284→
   285→    // ─ 상단 노란빛 그라데이션 ────────────────────────────────────
   286→    this.legacyTopGlow = scene.add.graphics();
   287→    this.legacyTopGlow.fillGradientStyle(0xffee00, 0xffee00, 0xffee00, 0xffee00, 0.28, 0.28, 0, 0);
   288→    this.legacyTopGlow.fillRect(0, 0, 400, 220);
   289→    this.legacyTopGlow.setDepth(148);
   290→
   291→    this.legacyPulseTween = scene.tweens.add({
   292→      targets: [this.legacyTopGlow], alpha: 0.55,
   293→      duration: 600, yoyo: true, repeat: -1, ease: 'Sine.easeInOut',
   294→    });
   295→
   296→    // ─ 레거시 모드 황금 빗줄기 (repeat: -1, 10초 후 remove로 정리) ─
   297→    this.legacyRainTimer = scene.time.addEvent({
   298→      delay: 120,
   299→      repeat: -1,
   300→      callback: () => { this.spawnRainStar(scene, LegacyAbility.RAIN_COLS_LEGACY, 0.75, 1.00, 3); },
   301→    });
   302→
   303→    // 레거시 모드 종료
   304→    this.legacyEndTimer = scene.time.delayedCall(LEGACY_PARAMS.legacyDuration, () => {
   305→      this.legacyModeActive = false;
   306→      this.legacyRainTimer?.remove();
   307→      this.legacyRainTimer = undefined;
   308→      this.legacyPulseTween?.stop();
   309→      this.legacyPulseTween = undefined;
   310→      scene.tweens.add({
   311→        targets: [this.legacyTopGlow], alpha: 0, duration: 400, ease: 'Quad.easeOut',
   312→        onComplete: () => {
   313→          this.legacyTopGlow?.destroy();
   314→          this.legacyTopGlow = undefined;
   315→        },
   316→      });
   317→
   318→    });
   319→  }
   320→
   321→  // ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
   322→  // D: 황금 빗줄기
   323→  // ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
   324→
   325→  private startFeverRain(api: GameSceneAPI): void {
   326→    const scene  = api.scene;
   327→    const REPEAT = Math.floor(LEGACY_PARAMS.feverDuration / 140) - 1;
   328→
   329→    this.startFeverTimer = scene.time.addEvent({
   330→      delay: 140,
   331→      repeat: REPEAT,
   332→      callback: () => { this.spawnRainStar(scene, LegacyAbility.RAIN_COLS_FEVER, 0.60, 0.90, 2); },
   333→    });
   334→  }
   335→
   336→  /** 빗줄기 별 1개 생성 — startFeverRain / legacyRainTimer 공용 */
   337→  private spawnRainStar(
   338→    scene: Phaser.Scene,
   339→    cols: readonly number[],
   340→    aMin: number,
   341→    aMax: number,
   342→    count: number,
   343→  ): void {
   344→    for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
   345→      const gfx = scene.add.graphics();
   346→      const s   = Phaser.Math.FloatBetween(2.5, 5.5);
   347→      const col = Phaser.Utils.Array.GetRandom(cols as number[]) as number;
   348→
   349→      gfx.fillStyle(col, Phaser.Math.FloatBetween(aMin, aMax));
   350→      gfx.fillPoints(LegacyAbility.starPts(s), true);
   351→      gfx.fillStyle(0xffffff, aMax > 0.85 ? 0.65 : 0.50); // 레거시 모드는 하이라이트도 강하게
   352→      gfx.fillCircle(0, 0, s * 0.32);
   353→
   354→      gfx.setPosition(Phaser.Math.Between(10, 390), -15);
   355→      gfx.setRotation(Phaser.Math.DegToRad(Phaser.Math.Between(0, 360)));
   356→      gfx.setDepth(85);
   357→
   358→      scene.tweens.add({
   359→        targets:  gfx,
   360→        y:        gfx.y + Phaser.Math.Between(450, 600),
   361→        x:        gfx.x + Phaser.Math.Between(-25, 25),
   362→        rotation: gfx.rotation + Phaser.Math.FloatBetween(1.5, 3.0),
   363→        alpha:    0,
   364→        duration: Phaser.Math.Between(2200, 3600),
   365→        ease:     'Linear',
   366→        onComplete: () => { gfx.destroy(); },
   367→      });
   368→    }
   369→  }
   370→
   371→  // ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
   372→  // 불태우기 로직
   373→  // ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
   374→
   375→  private burnRandomPoops(api: GameSceneAPI, count: number): void {
   376→    const visible = api.poops.getChildren().filter(
   377→      c => c.active && (c as Phaser.Physics.Arcade.Sprite).y > -10,
   378→    ) as Phaser.Physics.Arcade.Sprite[];
   379→
   380→    if (visible.length === 0) return;
   381→
   382→    // Partial Fisher-Yates: count개만 앞으로 뽑아내고 나머지 순회 생략
   383→    const pool = visible.slice();
   384→    const take = Math.min(count, pool.length);
   385→    for (let i = 0; i < take; i++) {
   386→      const j = i + Math.floor(Math.random() * (pool.length - i));
   387→      [pool[i], pool[j]] = [pool[j], pool[i]];
   388→    }
   389→    const targets = pool.slice(0, take);
   390→
   391→    this.drawFireTrail(
   392→      api.scene,
   393→      api.player.x, api.player.y,
   394→      targets.map(p => ({ x: p.x, y: p.y })),
   395→    );
   396→
   397→    for (const poop of targets) {
   398→      if (!poop.active) continue;
   399→      this.playBurnEffect(api.scene, poop.x, poop.y);
   400→      // 풀에 반환 (destroy 대신 — GC 압박 방지)
   401→      poop.setActive(false).setVisible(false);
   402→      const body = poop.body as Phaser.Physics.Arcade.Body;
   403→      if (body) { body.setVelocity(0, 0); body.setEnable(false); }
   404→      api.updateScore(10);
   405→    }
   406→  }
   407→
   408→  // ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
   409→  // 리소스 정리 (게임 오버 시 GameScene에서 호출)
   410→  // ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
   411→
   412→  override onDestroy(_api: GameSceneAPI): void {
   413→    this.startFeverEndTimer?.remove();
   414→    this.startFeverTimer?.remove();
   415→    this.legacyEndTimer?.remove();
   416→    this.legacyRainTimer?.remove();
   417→    this.legacyPulseTween?.stop();
   418→    this.legacyTopGlow?.destroy();
   419→  }
   420→
   421→  // ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
   422→  // 불꽃 소멸 이펙트
   423→  // ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
   424→
   425→  private playBurnEffect(scene: Phaser.Scene, cx: number, cy: number): void {
   426→    const flash = scene.add.graphics();
   427→    flash.fillStyle(0xff7700, 0.60); flash.fillCircle(0, 0, 17);
   428→    flash.fillStyle(0xffee88, 0.45); flash.fillCircle(0, 0, 9);
   429→    flash.setPosition(cx, cy).setDepth(202);
   430→    scene.tweens.add({
   431→      targets: flash, scaleX: 2.8, scaleY: 2.8, alpha: 0,
   432→      duration: 190, ease: 'Quad.easeOut',
   433→      onComplete: () => { flash.destroy(); },
   434→    });
   435→
   436→    for (let i = 0; i < 6; i++) {
   437→      const gfx = scene.add.graphics();
   438→      const w   = Phaser.Math.Between(5, 11);
   439→      const h   = Phaser.Math.Between(14, 27);
   440→
   441→      gfx.fillStyle(0xaa1100, 0.25); gfx.fillPoints(LegacyAbility.flamePts(w,        h       ), true);
   442→      gfx.fillStyle(0xff4400, 0.48); gfx.fillPoints(LegacyAbility.flamePts(w * 0.70, h * 0.76), true);
   443→      gfx.fillStyle(0xffcc00, 0.68); gfx.fillPoints(LegacyAbility.flamePts(w * 0.40, h * 0.50), true);
   444→
   445→      gfx.setPosition(
   446→        cx + Phaser.Math.Between(-15, 15),
   447→        cy + Phaser.Math.Between(-10, 10),
   448→      );
   449→      gfx.setRotation(Phaser.Math.DegToRad(Phaser.Math.Between(-28, 28)));
   450→      gfx.setDepth(200);
   451→
   452→      const rise = Phaser.Math.Between(48, 88);
   453→      const dur  = Phaser.Math.Between(500, 900);
   454→
   455→      scene.tweens.add({
   456→        targets: gfx, y: gfx.y - rise * 0.38, scaleX: 1.45, scaleY: 1.45,
   457→        duration: dur * 0.35, ease: 'Quad.easeOut',
   458→        onComplete: () => {
   459→          scene.tweens.add({
   460→            targets: gfx, y: gfx.y - rise * 0.62, scaleX: 0, scaleY: 0, alpha: 0,
   461→            duration: dur * 0.65, ease: 'Quad.easeIn',
   462→            onComplete: () => { gfx.destroy(); },
   463→          });
   464→        },
   465→      });
   466→    }
   467→
   468→    const EMBER_COLS = [0xff5500, 0xff8800, 0xffbb00];
   469→    for (let i = 0; i < 5; i++) {
   470→      const gfx = scene.add.graphics();
   471→      const s   = Phaser.Math.Between(2, 5);
   472→      const col = Phaser.Utils.Array.GetRandom(EMBER_COLS) as number;
   473→
   474→      gfx.fillStyle(col, 0.18);
   475→      gfx.fillPoints([{ x: 0, y: -s * 1.9 }, { x: s * 1.9, y: 0 }, { x: 0, y: s * 1.9 }, { x: -s * 1.9, y: 0 }], true);
   476→      gfx.fillStyle(0xffee88, 0.65);
   477→      gfx.fillPoints([{ x: 0, y: -s }, { x: s, y: 0 }, { x: 0, y: s }, { x: -s, y: 0 }], true);
   478→
   479→      gfx.setPosition(
   480→        cx + Phaser.Math.Between(-18, 18),
   481→        cy + Phaser.Math.Between(-18, 18),
   482→      ).setDepth(201);
   483→
   484→      const rad  = Phaser.Math.DegToRad(Phaser.Math.Between(185, 355));
   485→      const dist = Phaser.Math.Between(28, 65);
   486→
   487→      scene.tweens.add({
   488→        targets: gfx,
   489→        x: gfx.x + Math.cos(rad) * dist,
   490→        y: gfx.y + Math.sin(rad) * dist - Phaser.Math.Between(18, 40),
   491→        rotation: Phaser.Math.DegToRad(Phaser.Math.Between(200, 400)),
   492→        alpha: 0, scaleX: 0, scaleY: 0,
   493→        duration: Phaser.Math.Between(650, 1050),
   494→        delay:    Phaser.Math.Between(0, 220),
   495→        ease:     'Sine.easeOut',
   496→        onComplete: () => { gfx.destroy(); },
   497→      });
   498→    }
   499→
   500→    for (let i = 0; i < 2; i++) {
   501→      const gfx = scene.add.graphics();
   502→      gfx.fillStyle(0x331100, 0.28);
   503→      gfx.fillCircle(0, 0, Phaser.Math.Between(6, 13));
   504→      gfx.setPosition(
   505→        cx + Phaser.Math.Between(-12, 12),
   506→        cy + Phaser.Math.Between(-10, 4),
   507→      ).setDepth(199);
   508→
   509→      scene.tweens.add({
   510→        targets: gfx,
   511→        y:       gfx.y - Phaser.Math.Between(45, 85),
   512→        x:       gfx.x + Phaser.Math.Between(-14, 14),
   513→        scaleX:  2.4, scaleY: 2.4, alpha: 0,
   514→        duration: Phaser.Math.Between(850, 1350),
   515→        delay:    Phaser.Math.Between(80, 380),
   516→        ease:     'Sine.easeOut',
   517→        onComplete: () => { gfx.destroy(); },
   518→      });
   519→    }
   520→  }
   521→}

     1→import Phaser from 'phaser';
     2→import { BaseAbility } from './BaseAbility';
     3→import type { GameSceneAPI } from './types';
     4→import { MAEHWA_PARAMS } from '../config/abilityParams';
     5→
     6→/**
     7→ * 매화 (SR) — 이동속도 버프 / 점수마다 칼 베기
     8→ * 수치: MAEHWA_PARAMS 참조
     9→ */
    10→export class MaehwaAbility extends BaseAbility {
    11→  private lastMaehwaScore = 0;
    12→
    13→  override getPlayerSpeedBonus(): number {
    14→    return MAEHWA_PARAMS.speedBonus;
    15→  }
    16→
    17→  // ★2+: 특수 똥 수집 시 추가 점수 (MAEHWA_PARAMS.awake2SpecialBonus)
    18→  override onCollectSpecial(_type: import('./types').SpecialPoopType): number {
    19→    return this.awakeningLevel >= 2 ? MAEHWA_PARAMS.awake2SpecialBonus : 0;
    20→  }
    21→
    22→  override onScoreMilestone(score: number, api: GameSceneAPI): void {
    23→    if (score % MAEHWA_PARAMS.slashInterval === 0 && score > this.lastMaehwaScore) {
    24→      this.lastMaehwaScore = score;
    25→      this.slashClosestPoops(MAEHWA_PARAMS.slashCount, api);
    26→    }
    27→  }
    28→
    29→  private slashClosestPoops(count: number, api: GameSceneAPI): void {
    30→    const active = api.poops
    31→      .getChildren()
    32→      .filter(p => {
    33→        const sp = p as Phaser.Physics.Arcade.Sprite;
    34→        return sp.active && sp.y < api.player.y;
    35→      }) as Phaser.Physics.Arcade.Sprite[];
    36→
    37→    active.sort((a, b) => {
    38→      const da = Phaser.Math.Distance.Between(api.player.x, api.player.y, a.x, a.y);
    39→      const db = Phaser.Math.Distance.Between(api.player.x, api.player.y, b.x, b.y);
    40→      return da - db;
    41→    });
    42→
    43→    active.slice(0, count).forEach(poop => {
    44→      this.drawSlashEffect(poop.x, poop.y, api);
    45→      this.spawnPetals(poop.x, poop.y, api);
    46→      poop.destroy();
    47→    });
    48→  }
    49→
    50→  /** 가운데가 넓은 대각선(↗) 칼날 이펙트 */
    51→  private drawSlashEffect(cx: number, cy: number, api: GameSceneAPI): void {
    52→    const gfx = api.scene.add.graphics().setDepth(120);
    53→
    54→    // 칼날: fillPoints로 ↗ 방향 마름모꼴 (끝은 뾰족, 중앙은 넓음)
    55→    const len = 26; // 반길이
    56→    const wid = 7;  // 중앙 최대 반폭
    57→    // ↗ 방향 slash: (cx-len, cy+len) → (cx+len, cy-len) 축
    58→    gfx.fillStyle(0xff3366, 0.9);
    59→    gfx.fillPoints([
    60→      new Phaser.Geom.Point(cx - len, cy + len),          // 좌하 끝
    61→      new Phaser.Geom.Point(cx - wid * 0.4, cy + wid),    // 좌상 측면
    62→      new Phaser.Geom.Point(cx + len, cy - len),          // 우상 끝
    63→      new Phaser.Geom.Point(cx + wid * 0.4, cy - wid),    // 우하 측면
    64→    ], true);
    65→
    66→    // 외곽 흰색 글로우
    67→    gfx.lineStyle(3, 0xffffff, 0.5);
    68→    gfx.lineBetween(cx - len, cy + len, cx + len, cy - len);
    69→
    70→    api.scene.tweens.add({
    71→      targets: gfx,
    72→      alpha: 0,
    73→      duration: 300,
    74→      onComplete: () => gfx.destroy(),
    75→    });
    76→  }
    77→
    78→  /** 붉은 매화 잎 여러 개 낙화 */
    79→  private spawnPetals(cx: number, cy: number, api: GameSceneAPI): void {
    80→    const PETAL_COUNT = 7;
    81→    for (let i = 0; i < PETAL_COUNT; i++) {
    82→      const petal = api.scene.add.graphics().setDepth(121);
    83→      // 초기 위치: 파괴된 똥 주변 랜덤
    84→      const startX = cx + Phaser.Math.Between(-12, 12);
    85→      const startY = cy + Phaser.Math.Between(-12, 12);
    86→      petal.setPosition(startX, startY);
    87→
    88→      // 작은 타원형 꽃잎
    89→      const petalW = Phaser.Math.Between(4, 7);
    90→      const petalH = Phaser.Math.Between(7, 11);
    91→      petal.fillStyle(0xff2255, 0.85 + Math.random() * 0.15);
    92→      petal.fillEllipse(0, 0, petalW, petalH);
    93→      petal.setAngle(Phaser.Math.Between(0, 360));
    94→
    95→      // 바람에 날리며 낙하
    96→      api.scene.tweens.add({
    97→        targets: petal,
    98→        x: startX + Phaser.Math.Between(-25, 25),
    99→        y: startY + Phaser.Math.Between(30, 60),
   100→        angle: petal.angle + Phaser.Math.Between(-120, 120),
   101→        alpha: 0,
   102→        duration: Phaser.Math.Between(400, 700),
   103→        ease: 'Sine.easeIn',
   104→        onComplete: () => petal.destroy(),
   105→      });
   106→    }
   107→  }
   108→}
   109→

     3→/** GameScene이 CharacterAbility에 노출하는 API */
     4→export interface GameSceneAPI {

    35→export interface CharacterAbility {

    50→
    51→  /** 특수 똥 수집 시 추가 보너스 점수 반환 (없으면 0) */
    52→  onCollectSpecial(type: SpecialPoopType): number;

    54→  /** 금·다이아 스폰 시 낙하 속도 감소량 반환 (루트: 40) */

   745→    poop: Phaser.Physics.Arcade.Sprite,
   746→    type: import('../abilities/types').SpecialPoopType,
   747→    baseScore: number,
   748→    emoji: string,
   749→    color: string,
   750→    counterIncrement: () => void,
   751→  ) {
   752→    if (this.gameOver) return;
   753→    // 풀에 반환 (destroy 대신 recycle — GC 압박 방지)
   754→    (poop as unknown as { recycle(): void }).recycle();
   755→    counterIncrement();
   756→    const bonus = this.ability.onCollectSpecial(type) + (this.activeSynergy?.collectBonus ?? 0);
   757→    const total = baseScore + bonus;
   758→    this.updateScore(total);
   759→    if (!this.isFeverTime) {
   760→      const suffix = bonus > 0 ? ` (+${bonus})` : '';
   761→      const t = this.add.text(200, 100, `${emoji} +${total}점!${suffix} ${emoji}`, {
   762→        fontSize: '28px', color, fontStyle: 'bold',
   763→        stroke: '#000', strokeThickness: 4,
   764→      }).setOrigin(0.5);
   765→      this.time.delayedCall(1000, () => t.destroy());
   766→    }
   767→  }
   768→
   769→  private handleTopazCollected(poop: Phaser.Physics.Arcade.Sprite) {
   770→    this.handleSpecialCollected(poop, 'topaz', 80, '⭐', '#FFC300', () => { this.topazCollected++; });
   771→  }
   772→
   773→  private collectTopazPoop(
   774→    _player: Phaser.Types.Physics.Arcade.GameObjectWithBody | Phaser.Tilemaps.Tile,
   775→    _topazPoop: Phaser.Types.Physics.Arcade.GameObjectWithBody | Phaser.Tilemaps.Tile
   776→  ) {
   777→    this.handleTopazCollected(_topazPoop as TopazPoop);
   778→  }
   779→
   780→  private handleRainbowCollected(poop: Phaser.Physics.Arcade.Sprite) {
   781→    this.handleSpecialCollected(poop, 'rainbow', 90, '🌈', '#FF00FF', () => { this.rainbowCollected++; });
   782→  }
   783→
   784→  private collectRainbowPoop(
   785→    _player: Phaser.Types.Physics.Arcade.GameObjectWithBody | Phaser.Tilemaps.Tile,
   786→    _rainbowPoop: Phaser.Types.Physics.Arcade.GameObjectWithBody | Phaser.Tilemaps.Tile
   787→  ) {
   788→    this.handleRainbowCollected(_rainbowPoop as RainbowPoop);
   789→  }

> AGENT

이제 모든 이슈를 파악했습니다. 수정을 적용하겠습니다.

`★ Insight ─────────────────────────────────────`
**핵심 설계 문제: `recycle()` 패턴이 파편화되어 있음**
- `Poop`과 `PoolablePoopBase`가 동일한 `recycle()`/`update()`를 각자 정의
- 4개 파일에서 인라인 비활성화 코드가 copy-paste
- `as unknown as { recycle(): void }` 더블캐스트는 타입 안전성 결여

**해결 전략:** `Poop` → `PoolablePoopBase` 상속으로 통일, 모든 곳에서 `recycle()` 호출
`─────────────────────────────────────────────────`

> AGENT

`★ Insight ─────────────────────────────────────`
**수정 결과 요약 (38 insertions, 37 deletions — 코드 양 변화 없이 품질 개선):**
1. `Poop` → `PoolablePoopBase` 상속: 중복 `recycle()`/`update()` 제거
2. `as unknown as { recycle(): void }` → `as PoolablePoopBase`: 타입 안전한 캐스트
3. HackerAbility/MaehwaAbility: `destroy()` → `recycle()` (풀 영구 축소 버그 수정)
4. 모든 인라인 비활성화 → `recycle()` 호출로 통일 (DRY)
5. `PoolablePoopBase.update()`에 `if (!this.active) return` 추가 (프레임당 ~60회 불필요 호출 방지)
`─────────────────────────────────────────────────`

코드가 깨끗합니다. 특히 **HackerAbility/MaehwaAbility의 `destroy()` 버그**가 가장 중요한 수정이었습니다 — 이것이 장시간 플레이 시 풀이 점진적으로 축소되면서 새 객체를 계속 생성하게 만들어 GC 압박의 직접적 원인이었습니다.
