    89→  // 카드 그리드 공유 리소스
    90→  private coresGfx!: Phaser.GameObjects.Graphics;
    91→  private maskGfx!: Phaser.GameObjects.Graphics;
    92→
    93→  // 상세 정보 패널
    94→  private detailPanel: Phaser.GameObjects.Container | null = null;
    95→  private infoPanel: Phaser.GameObjects.Container | null = null;
    96→  private detailVideo: Phaser.GameObjects.Video | null = null;
    97→  private fitVideoTimers: Phaser.Time.TimerEvent[] = [];
    98→
    99→  constructor() {
   100→    super('CharacterSelectScene');
   101→  }
   102→
   103→  preload() {
   104→    for (const char of CHARACTERS) {
   105→      if (!this.textures.exists(char.imageKey)) {
   106→        this.load.image(char.imageKey, char.imagePath);
   107→      }
   108→      if (!this.textures.exists(char.illustKey)) {
   109→        this.load.image(char.illustKey, char.illustPath);
   110→      }
   111→      if (char.videoKey && char.videoPath && !this.cache.video.exists(char.videoKey)) {
   112→        this.load.video(char.videoKey, char.videoPath);
   113→      }
   114→    }
   115→    // 배경화면 bgKey(세로 이미지) 사전 로드 — 표시 대상 3종만
   116→    for (const wp of WALLPAPERS.filter(w => AVAILABLE_WP_SET.has(w.id))) {
   117→      if (!this.textures.exists(wp.bgKey)) {
   118→        this.load.image(wp.bgKey, wp.bgPath);
   119→      }
   120→    }
   121→  }
   122→
   123→  create() {
   124→    super.create();
   125→
   126→    this.selectedId = getSelectedCharacter();
   127→    this.ownedIds = getOwnedCharacters();
   128→    this.ownedWpIds = getOwnedWallpapers();
   129→    this.selectedWpId = getSelectedWallpaper();
   130→    this.activeTab = 'character';
   131→    this.scrollOffset = 0;
   132→    this.hasDragged = false;
   133→    this.hasPointerDownInScene = false;
   134→    this.cardHighlights.clear();
   135→    this.wpHighlights.clear();
   136→
   137→    // 동기화 전 각성 수치 스냅샷 (동기화 후 변화 감지용)
   138→    this._preSyncDupCounts = new Map(
   139→      CHARACTERS
   140→        .filter(c => this.ownedIds.includes(c.id) && c.grade !== '등급외')
   141→        .map(c => [c.id, getDuplicateCount(c.id)])
   142→    );
   143→
   144→    // 서버 DB와 동기화 — 소유 목록 or 각성 수치가 바뀐 경우 씬 재시작해서 카드 갱신
   145→    syncOwnedCharacters().then(synced => {
   146→      if (!this.scene.isActive()) return;
   147→      const ownedChanged = synced.length !== this.ownedIds.length ||
   148→        synced.some(id => !this.ownedIds.includes(id));
   149→      const awakeChanged = CHARACTERS.some(c =>
   150→        synced.includes(c.id) && c.grade !== '등급외' &&
   151→        getDuplicateCount(c.id) !== this._preSyncDupCounts.get(c.id)
   152→      );
   153→      if (ownedChanged || awakeChanged) this.scene.restart();
   154→    }).catch(() => { /* 네트워크 오류 시 로컬 상태 유지 */ });
   155→
   156→    // 배경화면 동기화 (비동기, UI 갱신 없이 진행 — 다음 방문 시 반영)
   157→    syncOwnedWallpapers().then(synced => {
   158→      if (!this.scene.isActive()) return;
   159→      const wpChanged = synced.length !== this.ownedWpIds.length ||
   160→        synced.some(id => !this.ownedWpIds.includes(id));
   161→      if (wpChanged) {
   162→        this.ownedWpIds = synced;
   163→        if (this.activeTab === 'wallpaper') this.rebuildGrid();
   164→      }
   165→    }).catch(() => { /* 네트워크 오류 시 로컬 상태 유지 */ });
   166→
   167→    const selectedDef = CHARACTERS.find(c => c.id === this.selectedId) ?? CHARACTERS[0];
   168→
   169→    // ── 배경: 선택된 캐릭터 일러스트 ───────────────────────────────────
   170→    this.bgImage = this.add.image(200, 300, selectedDef.illustKey);
   171→    this.bgImage.setDisplaySize(400, 600);
   172→
   173→    // ── 헤더 (고정) ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────
   174→    this.add.text(200, 30, '수집', {
   175→      fontSize: '22px',
   176→      color: '#ffffff',
   177→      fontStyle: 'bold',
   178→      stroke: '#000',
   179→      strokeThickness: 4,
   180→    }).setOrigin(0.5);
   181→
   182→    // ── 탭 버튼 ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
   183→    const TAB_Y = 60;
   184→    const TAB_W = 160;
   185→    const TAB_H = 30;
   186→
   187→    this.charTabBtnBg = this.add.rectangle(110, TAB_Y, TAB_W, TAB_H, 0x1144bb)
   188→      .setStrokeStyle(1.5, 0x4488ff)
   189→      .setInteractive({ useHandCursor: true });
   190→    this.charTabLabel = this.add.text(110, TAB_Y, '캐릭터', {
   191→      fontSize: '14px', color: '#ffffff', fontStyle: 'bold',
   192→    }).setOrigin(0.5);
   193→
   194→    this.wpTabBtnBg = this.add.rectangle(290, TAB_Y, TAB_W, TAB_H, 0x222222)
   195→      .setStrokeStyle(1.5, 0x555555)
   196→      .setInteractive({ useHandCursor: true });
   197→    this.wpTabLabel = this.add.text(290, TAB_Y, '배경화면', {
   198→      fontSize: '14px', color: '#888888', fontStyle: 'bold',
   199→    }).setOrigin(0.5);
   200→
   201→    this.charTabBtnBg.on('pointerup', () => {
   202→      if (this.detailPanel) return;
   203→      this.switchTab('character');
   204→    });
   205→    this.wpTabBtnBg.on('pointerup', () => {
   206→      if (this.detailPanel) return;
   207→      this.switchTab('wallpaper');
   208→    });
   209→
   210→    // 구분선
   211→    this.add.rectangle(200, 80, 380, 1, 0x444444);
   212→
   213→    // 현재 선택 상태 표시
   214→    this.headerNameText = this.add.text(200, 88, `현재: ${selectedDef.name}`, {
   215→      fontSize: '13px',
   216→      color: selectedDef.gradeColor,
   217→      stroke: '#000',
   218→      strokeThickness: 3,
   219→    }).setOrigin(0.5);
   220→
   221→    // ── 스크롤 가능한 카드 컨테이너 ─────────────────────────────────────
   222→    this.cardsContainer = this.add.container(0, 0);
   223→
   224→    this.buildCharacterGrid();
   225→
   226→    // 스크롤 최대 범위 계산 (buildCharacterGrid 내부에서도 설정되지만 여기서도 초기화)
   227→    const totalRows = Math.ceil(CHARACTERS.length / COLS);
   228→    const contentBottom = GRID_TOP + (totalRows - 1) * (CARD_H + GAP_Y) + CARD_H + 10;
   229→    this.maxScrollOffset = Math.max(0, contentBottom - SCROLL_BOTTOM);
   230→
   231→    // 카드 영역 마스크 (스크롤 영역 밖 숨김)
   232→    // this.make: display list에 추가되지 않으므로 shutdown 시 직접 정리 필요
   233→    this.maskGfx = this.make.graphics({ x: 0, y: 0 });
   234→    this.maskGfx.fillStyle(0xffffff);
   235→    this.maskGfx.fillRect(0, SCROLL_TOP, 400, SCROLL_BOTTOM - SCROLL_TOP);
   236→    this.cardsContainer.setMask(this.maskGfx.createGeometryMask());
   237→
   238→    // ── 드래그 스크롤 입력 ───────────────────────────────────────────────
   239→    this.input.on('pointerdown', (p: Phaser.Input.Pointer) => {
   240→      this.hasPointerDownInScene = true;
   241→      if (this.detailPanel) return; // 상세 패널 열려있으면 스크롤 무시
   242→      if (p.y < SCROLL_TOP || p.y > SCROLL_BOTTOM) return;
   243→      this.pointerDownY = p.y;
   244→      this.pointerDownScrollY = this.scrollOffset;
   245→      this.hasDragged = false;
   246→    });
   247→
   248→    this.input.on('pointermove', (p: Phaser.Input.Pointer) => {
   249→      if (this.detailPanel) return; // 상세 패널 열려있으면 스크롤 무시
   250→      if (!p.isDown) return;
   251→      const dy = this.pointerDownY - p.y;
   252→      if (Math.abs(dy) > 5) {
   253→        this.hasDragged = true;
   254→        this.scrollOffset = Phaser.Math.Clamp(
   255→          this.pointerDownScrollY + dy,
   256→          0,
   257→          this.maxScrollOffset,
   258→        );
   259→        this.cardsContainer.setY(-this.scrollOffset);
   260→      }
   261→    });
   262→
   263→    this.input.on('pointerup', () => {
   264→      if (this.detailPanel) return; // 상세 패널 열려있으면 hasDragged 초기화 무시
   265→      // card pointerup 이벤트가 먼저 발생하므로 다음 프레임에 초기화
   266→      this.time.delayedCall(0, () => {
   267→        this.hasDragged = false;
   268→        this.hasPointerDownInScene = false;
   269→      });
   270→    });
   271→
   272→    // 마우스 휠
   273→    this.input.on(
   274→      'wheel',
   275→      (_p: Phaser.Input.Pointer, _go: unknown[], _dx: number, deltaY: number) => {
   276→        if (this.detailPanel) return; // 상세 패널 열려있으면 휠 스크롤 무시
   277→        this.scrollOffset = Phaser.Math.Clamp(
   278→          this.scrollOffset + deltaY * 0.5,
   279→          0,
   280→          this.maxScrollOffset,
   281→        );
   282→        this.cardsContainer.setY(-this.scrollOffset);
   283→      },
   284→    );
   285→
   286→    // ── 고정 UI ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
   287→    this.createBackButton();
   288→
   289→    // maskGfx는 display list 외부에 있으므로 씬 종료 시 직접 정리
   290→    this.events.once('shutdown', () => { this.maskGfx.destroy(); });
   291→  }
   292→
   293→  // ── 그리드 빌더 ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
   294→
   295→  private buildCharacterGrid() {
   296→    this.cardHighlights.clear();
   297→    this.coresGfx = this.add.graphics();
   298→    CHARACTERS.forEach((char, index) => {
   299→      const col = index % COLS;
   300→      const row = Math.floor(index / COLS);
   301→      const x = GRID_LEFT + col * (CARD_W + GAP_X) + CARD_W / 2;
   302→      const y = GRID_TOP + row * (CARD_H + GAP_Y) + CARD_H / 2;
   303→      this.createCharacterCard(char, x, y);
   304→    });
   305→    this.cardsContainer.add(this.coresGfx);
   306→
   307→    const totalRows = Math.ceil(CHARACTERS.length / COLS);
   308→    const contentBottom = GRID_TOP + (totalRows - 1) * (CARD_H + GAP_Y) + CARD_H + 10;
   309→    this.maxScrollOffset = Math.max(0, contentBottom - SCROLL_BOTTOM);
   310→  }
   311→
   312→  private buildWallpaperGrid() {
   313→    this.wpHighlights.clear();
   314→    // ownedWpIds: create() 초기화 + sync callback에서 직접 갱신
   315→    // selectedWpId: applyWallpaper()가 항상 동기 업데이트 → 재조회 불필요
   316→
   317→    const availableWps = WALLPAPERS.filter(w => AVAILABLE_WP_SET.has(w.id));
   318→    availableWps.forEach((wp, index) => {
   319→      const col = index % WP_COLS;
   320→      const row = Math.floor(index / WP_COLS);
   321→      const x = WP_GRID_LEFT + col * (WP_CARD_W + WP_GAP_X) + WP_CARD_W / 2;
   322→      const y = WP_GRID_TOP + row * (WP_CARD_H + WP_GAP_Y) + WP_CARD_H / 2;
   323→      this.createWallpaperCard(wp, x, y);
   324→    });
   325→
   326→    const totalRows = Math.ceil(availableWps.length / WP_COLS);
   327→    const contentBottom = WP_GRID_TOP + (totalRows - 1) * (WP_CARD_H + WP_GAP_Y) + WP_CARD_H + 10;
   328→    this.maxScrollOffset = Math.max(0, contentBottom - SCROLL_BOTTOM);
   329→  }
   330→
   331→  private switchTab(tab: 'character' | 'wallpaper') {
   332→    if (this.activeTab === tab) return;
   333→    this.activeTab = tab;
   334→    this.scrollOffset = 0;
   335→    this.hasDragged = false;
   336→    this.cardsContainer.setY(0);
   337→    this.updateBgForTab();
   338→
   339→    // 컨테이너 내 카드 오브젝트 전부 제거
   340→    this.cardsContainer.removeAll(true);
   341→    this.cardHighlights.clear();
   342→    this.wpHighlights.clear();
   343→
   344→    // 탭 버튼 스타일 갱신
   345→    if (tab === 'character') {
   346→      this.charTabBtnBg.setFillStyle(0x1144bb).setStrokeStyle(1.5, 0x4488ff);
   347→      this.charTabLabel.setColor('#ffffff');
   348→      this.wpTabBtnBg.setFillStyle(0x222222).setStrokeStyle(1.5, 0x555555);
   349→      this.wpTabLabel.setColor('#888888');
   350→
   351→      this.buildCharacterGrid();
   352→
   353→      // 헤더 텍스트 갱신
   354→      const def = CHARACTERS.find(c => c.id === this.selectedId) ?? CHARACTERS[0];
   355→      this.headerNameText.setText(`현재: ${def.name}`).setColor(def.gradeColor);
   356→    } else {
   357→      this.wpTabBtnBg.setFillStyle(0x1144bb).setStrokeStyle(1.5, 0x4488ff);
   358→      this.wpTabLabel.setColor('#ffffff');
   359→      this.charTabBtnBg.setFillStyle(0x222222).setStrokeStyle(1.5, 0x555555);
   360→      this.charTabLabel.setColor('#888888');
   361→
   362→      this.buildWallpaperGrid();
   363→
   364→      // 헤더 텍스트 갱신
   365→      const selWpDef = this.selectedWpId
   366→        ? WALLPAPERS.find(w => w.id === this.selectedWpId)
   367→        : null;
   368→      this.headerNameText
   369→        .setText(`배경: ${selWpDef?.name ?? '기본'}`)
   370→        .setColor(this.selectedWpId ? WP_ACCENT_HEX : '#888888');
   371→    }
   372→  }
   373→
   374→  /** 현재 탭에 맞게 배경 이미지 업데이트 */
   375→  private updateBgForTab() {
   376→    if (this.activeTab === 'character') {
   377→      const def = CHARACTERS.find(c => c.id === this.selectedId) ?? CHARACTERS[0];
   378→      this.bgImage.setTexture(def.illustKey).setDisplaySize(400, 600).setAlpha(1).clearTint();
   379→    } else {
   380→      const wpDef = this.selectedWpId
   381→        ? WALLPAPERS.find(w => w.id === this.selectedWpId)
   382→        : null;
   383→      if (wpDef && this.textures.exists(wpDef.bgKey)) {
   384→        this.bgImage.setTexture(wpDef.bgKey).setDisplaySize(400, 600).setAlpha(1).clearTint();
   385→      } else {
   386→        // 선택 배경 없음 → 캐릭터 일러스트를 어둡게 처리
   387→        const def = CHARACTERS.find(c => c.id === this.selectedId) ?? CHARACTERS[0];
   388→        this.bgImage.setTexture(def.illustKey).setDisplaySize(400, 600).setAlpha(0.2);
   389→      }
   390→    }
   391→  }
   392→
   393→  /** 서버 동기화 후 현재 탭을 내용 갱신 */
   394→  private rebuildGrid() {
   395→    this.cardsContainer.removeAll(true);
   396→    this.cardHighlights.clear();
   397→    this.wpHighlights.clear();
   398→    if (this.activeTab === 'character') {
   399→      this.buildCharacterGrid();
   400→    } else {
   401→      this.buildWallpaperGrid();
   402→    }
   403→  }
   404→
   405→  // ── 배경화면 카드 ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
   406→
   407→  private createWallpaperCard(wp: BackgroundDef, x: number, y: number) {
   408→    const isOwned = this.ownedWpIds.includes(wp.id);
   409→    const isSelected = this.selectedWpId === wp.id;
   410→
   411→    // 카드 배경
   412→    const cardBg = this.add.rectangle(x, y, WP_CARD_W, WP_CARD_H, 0x1a1a2e);
   413→    cardBg.setStrokeStyle(2, isOwned ? WP_ACCENT_INT : 0x333333);
   414→    this.cardsContainer.add(cardBg);
   415→
   416→    // 선택 하이라이트 (흰 테두리)
   417→    const highlight = this.add.rectangle(x, y, WP_CARD_W, WP_CARD_H, 0, 0);
   418→    highlight.setStrokeStyle(3, 0xffffff);
   419→    highlight.setVisible(isSelected);
   420→    this.wpHighlights.set(wp.id, highlight);
   421→    this.cardsContainer.add(highlight);
   422→
   423→    // 세로 이미지 (bgKey) — 카드 상단을 채우는 portrait 썸네일
   424→    if (this.textures.exists(wp.bgKey)) {
   425→      const thumb = this.add.image(x, y - 11, wp.bgKey)
   426→        .setDisplaySize(WP_CARD_W - 4, WP_CARD_H - 22);
   427→      if (!isOwned) { thumb.setTint(0x000000); thumb.setAlpha(0.5); }
   428→      this.cardsContainer.add(thumb);
   429→    }
   430→
   431→    // 미보유 자물쇠
   432→    if (!isOwned) {
   433→      const lock = this.add.text(x, y - 8, '🔒', { fontSize: '20px' }).setOrigin(0.5);
   434→      this.cardsContainer.add(lock);
   435→    }
   436→
   437→    // WP 배지 (우상단) — 기본 배경은 '기본', 가챠 배경은 'WP'
   438→    const isDefault = (DEFAULT_WP_IDS as readonly string[]).includes(wp.id);
   439→    const badgeText  = isDefault ? '기본' : 'WP';
   440→    const badgeColor = isDefault ? '#aaaaaa' : WP_ACCENT_HEX;
   441→    const badge = this.add.text(x + WP_CARD_W / 2 - 2, y - WP_CARD_H / 2 + 2, badgeText, {
   442→      fontSize: '9px', color: badgeColor, fontStyle: 'bold',
   443→      backgroundColor: '#000000cc', padding: { x: 3, y: 1 },
   444→    }).setOrigin(1, 0);
   445→    this.cardsContainer.add(badge);
   446→
   447→    // 하단 반투명 바 + 이름
   448→    const barY = y + WP_CARD_H / 2 - 11;
   449→    const bar = this.add.rectangle(x, barY, WP_CARD_W, 22, 0x000000, 0.75);
   450→    this.cardsContainer.add(bar);
   451→    const nameText = this.add.text(x, barY, wp.name, {
   452→      fontSize: '11px',
   453→      color: isOwned ? WP_ACCENT_HEX : '#444444',
   454→      fontStyle: 'bold',
   455→    }).setOrigin(0.5);
   456→    this.cardsContainer.add(nameText);
   457→
   458→    // 클릭 이벤트
   459→    cardBg.setInteractive({ useHandCursor: isOwned });
   460→    if (isOwned) {
   461→      cardBg.on('pointerover', () => cardBg.setFillStyle(0x252540));
   462→      cardBg.on('pointerout',  () => cardBg.setFillStyle(0x1a1a2e));
   463→    }
   464→    cardBg.on('pointerup', () => {
   465→      if (!this.hasDragged && this.hasPointerDownInScene) this.showWallpaperDetail(wp);
   466→    });
   467→  }
   468→
   469→  // ── 배경화면 상세 오버레이 ──────────────────────────────────────────────
   470→
   471→  private showWallpaperDetail(def: BackgroundDef): void {
   472→    this.hideCharacterDetail();
   473→
   474→    const isOwned = this.ownedWpIds.includes(def.id);
   475→    const isSelected = this.selectedWpId === def.id;
   476→
   477→    const panel = this.add.container(0, 0).setDepth(300);
   478→    this.detailPanel = panel;
   479→
   480→    // 클릭 차단
   481→    const blocker = this.add.rectangle(200, 300, 400, 600, 0x000000, 0).setInteractive();
   482→    panel.add(blocker);
   483→
   484→    // 배경화면 전체화면 미리보기
   485→    if (this.textures.exists(def.bgKey)) {
   486→      const bg = this.add.image(200, 300, def.bgKey).setDisplaySize(400, 600);
   487→      panel.add(bg);
   488→    } else {
   489→      const fallback = this.add.rectangle(200, 300, 400, 600, 0x050515);
   490→      const hint = this.add.text(200, 300, '이미지 없음\n(에셋 추가 필요)', {
   491→        fontSize: '16px', color: '#666666', align: 'center',
   492→      }).setOrigin(0.5);
   493→      panel.add(fallback);
   494→      panel.add(hint);
   495→    }
   496→
   497→    // 하단 그라디언트 오버레이
   498→    const grad = this.add.graphics();
   499→    grad.fillGradientStyle(0x000000, 0x000000, 0x000000, 0x000000, 0, 0, 0.8, 0.8);
   500→    grad.fillRect(0, 380, 400, 220);
   501→    panel.add(grad);
   502→
   503→    // ✕ 닫기 버튼
   504→    const closeBg = this.add.circle(372, 38, 22, 0x000000, 0.55)
   505→      .setInteractive({ useHandCursor: true });
   506→    const closeBtn = this.add.text(372, 38, '✕', { fontSize: '18px', color: '#cccccc' }).setOrigin(0.5);
   507→    closeBg.on('pointerover', () => { closeBg.setFillStyle(0x333333, 0.8); closeBtn.setColor('#ffffff'); });
   508→    closeBg.on('pointerout',  () => { closeBg.setFillStyle(0x000000, 0.55); closeBtn.setColor('#cccccc'); });
   509→    closeBg.on('pointerup',   () => this.hideCharacterDetail());
   510→    panel.add(closeBg);
   511→    panel.add(closeBtn);
   512→
   513→    // 이름
   514→    panel.add(this.add.text(24, 450, def.name, {
   515→      fontSize: '26px', color: '#ffffff', fontStyle: 'bold',
   516→      stroke: '#000000', strokeThickness: 5,
   517→    }));
   518→    panel.add(this.add.text(24, 494, def.description, {
   519→      fontSize: '12px', color: '#aaaaaa',
   520→      stroke: '#000000', strokeThickness: 3,
   521→      wordWrap: { width: 350 },
   522→    }));
   523→
   524→    // ── 버튼 2개 ──
   525→    const BTN_Y = 562;
   526→    const BTN_W = 168;
   527→    const BTN_H = 42;
   528→
   529→    if (isOwned) {
   530→      if (!isSelected) {
   531→        // [적용하기]
   532→        const applyBg = this.add.rectangle(108, BTN_Y, BTN_W, BTN_H, 0x115511)
   533→          .setStrokeStyle(2, WP_ACCENT_INT)
   534→          .setInteractive({ useHandCursor: true });
   535→        applyBg.on('pointerover', () => applyBg.setFillStyle(0x226622));
   536→        applyBg.on('pointerout',  () => applyBg.setFillStyle(0x115511));
   537→        applyBg.on('pointerup',   () => this.applyWallpaper(def.id));
   538→        panel.add(applyBg);
   539→        panel.add(this.add.text(108, BTN_Y, '✔  적용하기', {
   540→          fontSize: '14px', color: '#88ff88', fontStyle: 'bold',
   541→        }).setOrigin(0.5));
   542→      } else {
   543→        // [해제]
   544→        const removeBg = this.add.rectangle(108, BTN_Y, BTN_W, BTN_H, 0x331111)
   545→          .setStrokeStyle(2, 0x884444)
   546→          .setInteractive({ useHandCursor: true });
   547→        removeBg.on('pointerover', () => removeBg.setFillStyle(0x442222));
   548→        removeBg.on('pointerout',  () => removeBg.setFillStyle(0x331111));
   549→        removeBg.on('pointerup',   () => this.applyWallpaper(null));
   550→        panel.add(removeBg);
   551→        panel.add(this.add.text(108, BTN_Y, '✕  해제', {
   552→          fontSize: '14px', color: '#ff8888', fontStyle: 'bold',
   553→        }).setOrigin(0.5));
   554→      }
   555→    } else {
   556→      const lockBg = this.add.rectangle(108, BTN_Y, BTN_W, BTN_H, 0x1a1a1a)
   557→        .setStrokeStyle(1.5, 0x444444);
   558→      panel.add(lockBg);
   559→      panel.add(this.add.text(108, BTN_Y, '🔒  미보유', {
   560→        fontSize: '14px', color: '#555555',
   561→      }).setOrigin(0.5));
   562→    }
   563→
   564→    // [닫기] 버튼 (우측)
   565→    const closeBtnBg = this.add.rectangle(292, BTN_Y, BTN_W, BTN_H, 0x1a1a1a)
   566→      .setStrokeStyle(1.5, 0x555555)
   567→      .setInteractive({ useHandCursor: true });
   568→    closeBtnBg.on('pointerover', () => closeBtnBg.setFillStyle(0x2e2e2e));
   569→    closeBtnBg.on('pointerout',  () => closeBtnBg.setFillStyle(0x1a1a1a));
   570→    closeBtnBg.on('pointerup',   () => this.hideCharacterDetail());
   571→    panel.add(closeBtnBg);
   572→    panel.add(this.add.text(292, BTN_Y, '닫기', {
   573→      fontSize: '14px', color: '#cccccc', fontStyle: 'bold',
   574→    }).setOrigin(0.5));
   575→  }
   576→
   577→  private applyWallpaper(id: string | null) {
   578→    setSelectedWallpaper(id);
   579→    this.selectedWpId = id;
   580→
   581→    // 하이라이트 갱신
   582→    this.wpHighlights.forEach((rect, wpId) => {
   583→      rect.setVisible(wpId === id);
   584→    });
   585→
   586→    // 헤더 텍스트 갱신
   587→    const def = id ? WALLPAPERS.find(w => w.id === id) : null;
   588→    this.headerNameText
   589→      .setText(`배경: ${def?.name ?? '기본'}`)
   590→      .setColor(id ? WP_ACCENT_HEX : '#888888');
   591→
   592→    // 배경 이미지 즉시 반영
   593→    this.updateBgForTab();
   594→
   595→    this.hideCharacterDetail();
   596→  }
   597→
   598→  private createCharacterCard(char: CharacterDef, x: number, y: number) {
   599→    const isOwned = this.ownedIds.includes(char.id);
   600→    const isSelected = this.selectedId === char.id;
   601→    const gradeColorInt = parseInt(char.gradeColor.replace('#', ''), 16);
   602→
   603→    // 카드 배경
   604→    const cardBg = this.add.rectangle(x, y, CARD_W, CARD_H, 0x222222);
   605→    cardBg.setStrokeStyle(2, isOwned ? gradeColorInt : 0x444444);
   606→    this.cardsContainer.add(cardBg);
   607→
   608→    // 선택 하이라이트
   609→    const highlight = this.add.rectangle(x, y, CARD_W, CARD_H, 0, 0);
   610→    highlight.setStrokeStyle(3, 0xffffff);
   611→    highlight.setVisible(isSelected);
   612→    this.cardHighlights.set(char.id, highlight);
   613→    this.cardsContainer.add(highlight);
   614→
   615→    // 캐릭터 이미지
   616→    const img = this.add.image(x, y - 12, char.imageKey);
   617→    img.setDisplaySize(58, 85);
   618→    this.cardsContainer.add(img);
   619→
   620→    if (!isOwned) {
   621→      img.setTint(0x000000);
   622→      img.setAlpha(0.6);
   623→      const lock = this.add.text(x, y - 12, '🔒', { fontSize: '22px' }).setOrigin(0.5);
   624→      this.cardsContainer.add(lock);
   625→    }
   626→
   627→    // 등급 배지
   628→    const badge = this.add.text(x + CARD_W / 2 - 2, y - CARD_H / 2 + 2, char.grade, {
   629→      fontSize: '9px',
   630→      color: char.gradeColor,
   631→      fontStyle: 'bold',
   632→      backgroundColor: '#000000cc',
   633→      padding: { x: 3, y: 1 },
   634→    }).setOrigin(1, 0);
   635→    this.cardsContainer.add(badge);
   636→
   637→    // 각성 코어 (등급외 제외, 보유 캐릭터만) — 공유 coresGfx에 직접 그림
   638→    if (isOwned && char.grade !== '등급외') {
   639→      const dupCount   = getDuplicateCount(char.id);
   640→      const awakeLevel = getAwakeningLevel(char.grade, dupCount);
   641→      const coreY      = y + CARD_H / 2 - CORE_Y_OFFSET;
   642→
   643→      for (let i = 0; i < CORE_COUNT; i++) {
   644→        const cx = x + (i - 2) * CORE_GAP;
   645→        if (i < awakeLevel) {
   646→          // 충전된 코어: 외곽 글로우 + 내부 밝은 원
   647→          this.coresGfx.fillStyle(gradeColorInt, 0.25);
   648→          this.coresGfx.fillCircle(cx, coreY, CORE_GLOW_RADIUS);
   649→          this.coresGfx.fillStyle(gradeColorInt, 1);
   650→          this.coresGfx.fillCircle(cx, coreY, CORE_INNER_RADIUS);
   651→          this.coresGfx.fillStyle(0xffffff, 0.55);
   652→          this.coresGfx.fillCircle(cx - 1, coreY - 1, CORE_HIGHLIGHT_R);
   653→        } else {
   654→          // 빈 코어: 어두운 원 + 얇은 테두리
   655→          this.coresGfx.fillStyle(0x111111, 1);
   656→          this.coresGfx.fillCircle(cx, coreY, CORE_INNER_RADIUS);
   657→          this.coresGfx.lineStyle(1, gradeColorInt, 0.35);
   658→          this.coresGfx.strokeCircle(cx, coreY, CORE_INNER_RADIUS);
   659→        }
   660→      }
   661→    }
   662→
   663→    // 캐릭터 이름
   664→    const nameText = this.add.text(x, y + CARD_H / 2 - 16, char.name, {
   665→      fontSize: '12px',
   666→      color: isOwned ? '#ffffff' : '#555555',
   667→      fontStyle: 'bold',
   668→    }).setOrigin(0.5);
   669→    this.cardsContainer.add(nameText);
   670→
   671→    // 모든 카드 클릭 가능 (탭 → 상세 패널 열기)
   672→    cardBg.setInteractive({ useHandCursor: isOwned });
   673→
   674→    if (isOwned) {
   675→      cardBg.on('pointerover', () => {
   676→        if (this.selectedId !== char.id) cardBg.setFillStyle(0x333333);
   677→      });
   678→      cardBg.on('pointerout', () => {
   679→        if (this.selectedId !== char.id) cardBg.setFillStyle(0x222222);
   680→      });
   681→    }
   682→    // pointerup으로 상세 패널 열기 (드래그 스크롤과 구분, 이전 씬 bleed-through 방지)
   683→    cardBg.on('pointerup', () => {
   684→      if (!this.hasDragged && this.hasPointerDownInScene) this.showCharacterDetail(char.id);
   685→    });
   686→  }
   687→
   688→  private selectCharacter(id: string) {
   689→    // 이전 선택 하이라이트 제거
   690→    const prev = this.cardHighlights.get(this.selectedId);
   691→    if (prev) prev.setVisible(false);
   692→
   693→    this.selectedId = id;
   694→    setSelectedCharacter(id);
   695→
   696→    const next = this.cardHighlights.get(id);
   697→    if (next) next.setVisible(true);
   698→
   699→    // 헤더 직접 갱신 (씬 재시작 없이)
   700→    const def = CHARACTERS.find(c => c.id === id) ?? CHARACTERS[0];
   701→    this.headerNameText.setText(`현재: ${def.name}`);
   702→    this.headerNameText.setColor(def.gradeColor);
   703→    this.bgImage.setTexture(def.illustKey);
   704→  }
   705→
   706→  private showCharacterDetail(id: string): void {
   707→    this.hideCharacterDetail();
   708→
   709→    const def = CHARACTERS.find(c => c.id === id) ?? CHARACTERS[0];
   710→    const isOwned = this.ownedIds.includes(id);
   711→    const gradeColorInt = parseInt(def.gradeColor.replace('#', ''), 16);
   712→
   713→    const panel = this.add.container(0, 0).setDepth(300);
   714→    this.detailPanel = panel;
   715→
   716→    // ── 클릭 투과 차단 레이어 (카드 목록으로 이벤트 전달 방지)
   717→    const blocker = this.add.rectangle(200, 300, 400, 600, 0x000000, 0).setInteractive();
   718→    panel.add(blocker);
   719→
   720→    // ── 일러스트 전체 화면 ───────────────────────────────────────────
   721→    const illust = this.add.image(200, 300, def.illustKey).setDisplaySize(400, 600);
   722→    panel.add(illust);
   723→
   724→    // ── 비디오 (일러스트와 같은 위치, 처음엔 숨김) ─────────────────
   725→    const hasVideo = !!(def.videoKey && this.cache.video.exists(def.videoKey));
   726→    const videoObj = hasVideo
   727→      ? this.add.video(200, 300, def.videoKey!).setDisplaySize(400, 600).setVisible(false)
   728→      : null;
   729→    if (videoObj) {
   730→      panel.add(videoObj);
   731→      this.detailVideo = videoObj;
   732→    }
   733→
   734→    // ── 하단 그라디언트 (위 투명 → 아래 짙은 어둠) ─────────────────
   735→    // 비디오/일러스트 위, 버튼 아래에 위치하도록 여기서 추가
   736→    const grad = this.add.graphics();
   737→    grad.fillGradientStyle(0x000000, 0x000000, 0x000000, 0x000000, 0, 0, 0.78, 0.78);
   738→    grad.fillRect(0, 380, 400, 220);
   739→    panel.add(grad);
   740→
   741→    // ── ✕ 닫기 버튼 (우상단 플로팅) ────────────────────────────────
   742→    const closeBg = this.add.circle(372, 38, 22, 0x000000, 0.55)
   743→      .setInteractive({ useHandCursor: true });
   744→    const closeBtn = this.add.text(372, 38, '✕', {
   745→      fontSize: '18px', color: '#cccccc',
   746→    }).setOrigin(0.5);
   747→    closeBg.on('pointerover', () => { closeBg.setFillStyle(0x333333, 0.8); closeBtn.setColor('#ffffff'); });
   748→    closeBg.on('pointerout',  () => { closeBg.setFillStyle(0x000000, 0.55); closeBtn.setColor('#cccccc'); });
   749→    closeBg.on('pointerup',   () => this.hideCharacterDetail());
   750→    panel.add(closeBg);
   751→    panel.add(closeBtn);
   752→
   753→    // ── 하단 정보 영역 ───────────────────────────────────────────────
   754→    // 픽셀 스프라이트
   755→    const sprite = this.add.image(38, 515, def.imageKey).setDisplaySize(46, 64);
   756→    if (!isOwned) { sprite.setTint(0x222222).setAlpha(0.55); }
   757→    panel.add(sprite);
   758→
   759→    // 등급 배지
   760→    const gradeBadge = this.add.text(74, 492, def.grade, {
   761→      fontSize: '12px', color: def.gradeColor, fontStyle: 'bold',
   762→      stroke: '#000000', strokeThickness: 4,
   763→    });
   764→    panel.add(gradeBadge);
   765→
   766→    // 캐릭터 이름 (크게)
   767→    const nameText = this.add.text(74, 510, def.name, {
   768→      fontSize: '26px', color: '#ffffff', fontStyle: 'bold',
   769→      stroke: '#000000', strokeThickness: 5,
   770→    });
   771→    panel.add(nameText);
   772→
   773→    // ── 하단 버튼 2개 (나란히) ──────────────────────────────────────
   774→    const BTN_Y = 568;
   775→    const BTN_W = 168;
   776→    const BTN_H = 42;
   777→
   778→    // 📋 정보 보기 (좌)
   779→    const infoBg = this.add.rectangle(108, BTN_Y, BTN_W, BTN_H, 0x1a1a1a)
   780→      .setStrokeStyle(1.5, 0x555555)
   781→      .setInteractive({ useHandCursor: true });
   782→    infoBg.on('pointerover', () => infoBg.setFillStyle(0x2e2e2e));
   783→    infoBg.on('pointerout',  () => infoBg.setFillStyle(0x1a1a1a));
   784→    infoBg.on('pointerup',   () => this.showInfoPanel(def));
   785→    const infoLabel = this.add.text(108, BTN_Y, '📋  정보 보기', {
   786→      fontSize: '14px', color: '#cccccc', fontStyle: 'bold',
   787→    }).setOrigin(0.5);
   788→    panel.add(infoBg);
   789→    panel.add(infoLabel);
   790→
   791→    // ✔ 선택하기 / 🔒 미보유 (우)
   792→    if (isOwned) {
   793→      const selBg = this.add.rectangle(292, BTN_Y, BTN_W, BTN_H, 0x1144bb)
   794→        .setStrokeStyle(2, gradeColorInt)
   795→        .setInteractive({ useHandCursor: true });
   796→      selBg.on('pointerover', () => selBg.setFillStyle(0x2860dd));
   797→      selBg.on('pointerout',  () => selBg.setFillStyle(0x1144bb));
   798→      selBg.on('pointerup',   () => this.confirmSelect(id));
   799→      const selLabel = this.add.text(292, BTN_Y, '✔  선택하기', {
   800→        fontSize: '15px', color: '#ffffff', fontStyle: 'bold',
   801→        stroke: '#000000', strokeThickness: 2,
   802→      }).setOrigin(0.5);
   803→      panel.add(selBg);
   804→      panel.add(selLabel);
   805→    } else {
   806→      const lockBg = this.add.rectangle(292, BTN_Y, BTN_W, BTN_H, 0x1a1a1a)
   807→        .setStrokeStyle(1.5, 0x444444);
   808→      const lockLabel = this.add.text(292, BTN_Y, '🔒  미보유', {
   809→        fontSize: '14px', color: '#555555',
   810→      }).setOrigin(0.5);
   811→      panel.add(lockBg);
   812→      panel.add(lockLabel);
   813→    }
   814→
   815→    // ── 비디오 인터랙션 (비디오가 있는 캐릭터만) ────────────────────
   816→    if (hasVideo && videoObj) {
   817→      // 일러스트 탭 → 비디오 재생 (가챠와 동일한 fitVideo 방식)
   818→      // active 체크: ✕ 탭 시 패널 파괴 직후 이 핸들러가 동시에 발화하는 경우 방지
   819→      const startVideo = () => {
   820→        if (!illust.active || !videoObj?.active) return;
   821→        videoObj!.setVisible(true);
   822→        videoObj!.play(false);
   823→        this.fitVideoToPanel(videoObj!);
   824→        // 비디오 'play' 이벤트 = 실제 재생 시작 시점 → 그때 일러스트 숨김
   825→        // (즉시 숨기면 첫 프레임 렌더 전 갭에 카드 목록이 비침)
   826→        videoObj!.once('play', () => {
   827→          if (illust.active) illust.setVisible(false);
   828→        });
   829→      };
   830→      illust.setInteractive({ useHandCursor: true });
   831→      illust.on('pointerup', startVideo);
   832→
   833→      // 비디오 탭 또는 재생 완료 → 일러스트로 복귀
   834→      const stopVideo = () => {
   835→        videoObj!.stop();
   836→        videoObj!.setVisible(false);
   837→        illust.setVisible(true);
   838→      };
   839→      videoObj.setInteractive({ useHandCursor: true });
   840→      videoObj.on('pointerup', stopVideo);
   841→      videoObj.on('complete', stopVideo);
   842→    }
   843→  }
   844→
   845→  /** GachaScene.fitVideoToCanvas와 동일 — aspect ratio 유지하며 400×600 캔버스에 cover */
   846→  private fitVideoToPanel(vid: Phaser.GameObjects.Video): void {
   847→    const W = 400, H = 600;
   848→    vid.setDisplaySize(W, H);
   849→
   850→    const applyContain = () => {
   851→      // eslint-disable-next-line @typescript-eslint/no-explicit-any
   852→      const el: HTMLVideoElement | null = (vid as any).video ?? null;
   853→      const nw = el?.videoWidth  || 0;
   854→      const nh = el?.videoHeight || 0;
   855→      if (nw > 0 && nh > 0) {
   856→        const scale = Math.max(W / nw, H / nh);
   857→        vid.setDisplaySize(Math.round(nw * scale), Math.round(nh * scale));
   858→      }
   859→    };
   860→
   861→    vid.once('play', applyContain);
   862→    this.fitVideoTimers.push(this.time.delayedCall(100, applyContain));
   863→    this.fitVideoTimers.push(this.time.delayedCall(500, applyContain));
   864→  }
   865→
   866→  private hideCharacterDetail(): void {
   867→    // fitVideoToPanel의 pending 타이머 취소
   868→    for (const t of this.fitVideoTimers) t.remove();
   869→    this.fitVideoTimers = [];
   870→
   871→    // 비디오 명시 정지 및 HTMLVideoElement src 해제
   872→    if (this.detailVideo) {
   873→      this.detailVideo.stop();
   874→      // eslint-disable-next-line @typescript-eslint/no-explicit-any
   875→      const el: HTMLVideoElement | null = (this.detailVideo as any).video ?? null;
   876→      if (el) { el.src = ''; }
   877→      this.detailVideo = null;
   878→    }
   879→
   880→    this.hideInfoPanel();
   881→    this.detailPanel?.destroy();
   882→    this.detailPanel = null;
   883→  }
   884→
   885→  private confirmSelect(id: string): void {
   886→    this.selectCharacter(id);
   887→    this.hideCharacterDetail();
   888→  }
   889→
   890→  private showInfoPanel(def: CharacterDef): void {
   891→    this.hideInfoPanel();
   892→
   893→    const gradeColorInt = parseInt(def.gradeColor.replace('#', ''), 16);
   894→    const panel = this.add.container(0, 0).setDepth(400);
   895→    this.infoPanel = panel;
   896→
   897→    // ── 각성 보너스 계산 ──────────────────────────────────────────────
   898→    const dupCount   = getDuplicateCount(def.id);
   899→    const awakeLevel = getAwakeningLevel(def.grade, dupCount);
   900→    const awakeBonusLines: string[] = [];
   901→    if (def.grade !== '등급외') {
   902→      if (awakeLevel >= 1) {
   903→        const spd = def.id === 'maehwa' ? 5 : def.grade === 'UR' ? 15 : def.grade === 'SR' ? 10 : 5;
   904→        awakeBonusLines.push(`★1 각성: 이동속도 +${spd} px/s`);
   905→      }
   906→      if (awakeLevel >= 2) {
   907→        if (def.id === 'maehwa') {
   908→          awakeBonusLines.push('★2 각성: 특수 똥 수집 시 +5점');
   909→        } else {
   910→          const spd = def.grade === 'UR' ? 20 : def.grade === 'SR' ? 15 : 10;
   911→          awakeBonusLines.push(`★2 각성: 이동속도 +${spd} px/s (누적)`);
   912→        }
   913→      }
   914→    }
   915→    const awakeBonusText = awakeBonusLines.join('\n');
   916→
   917→    // 반투명 배경 (클릭 시 닫기)
   918→    const overlay = this.add.rectangle(200, 300, 400, 600, 0x000000, 0.80)
   919→      .setInteractive();
   920→    overlay.on('pointerup', () => this.hideInfoPanel());
   921→    panel.add(overlay);
   922→
   923→    // ── 카드 크기 계산 (텍스트 높이 선측정) ─────────────────────────
   924→    const B_LEFT = 30;
   925→    const CARD_W = 370;
   926→    const WRAP_W = CARD_W - 40; // 좌(15px)·우(25px) 여백 제외한 실제 텍스트 폭
   927→    const HEADER_H = 60;  // 스프라이트+이름+구분선 영역
   928→    const PAD_BOT  = 20;
   929→
   930→    const btMeasure = this.add.text(0, -999, def.basicEffect,    { fontSize: '13px', wordWrap: { width: WRAP_W } });
   931→    const stMeasure = this.add.text(0, -999, def.specialAbility, { fontSize: '13px', wordWrap: { width: WRAP_W } });
   932→    const abMeasure = awakeBonusText
   933→      ? this.add.text(0, -999, awakeBonusText, { fontSize: '12px', wordWrap: { width: WRAP_W } })
   934→      : null;
   935→    const abHeight  = abMeasure ? abMeasure.height + 8 : 0;
   936→    const cardH = HEADER_H + 18 + btMeasure.height + abHeight + 14 + 18 + stMeasure.height + PAD_BOT;
   937→    btMeasure.destroy();
   938→    stMeasure.destroy();
   939→    abMeasure?.destroy();
   940→
   941→    // 화면 중앙 배치 (상하 여백 각 70px 보장)
   942→    const cardTop = Math.max(70, Math.round((600 - cardH) / 2));
   943→    const card = this.add.rectangle(200, cardTop + cardH / 2, CARD_W, cardH, 0x111111)
   944→      .setStrokeStyle(2, gradeColorInt);
   945→    panel.add(card);
   946→
   947→    // ── 헤더: 픽셀 스프라이트 + 등급 + 이름 ─────────────────────────
   948→    const sprite = this.add.image(52, cardTop + 24, def.imageKey).setDisplaySize(38, 52);
   949→    panel.add(sprite);
   950→
   951→    const badge = this.add.text(82, cardTop + 8, def.grade, {
   952→      fontSize: '11px', color: def.gradeColor, fontStyle: 'bold',
   953→      stroke: '#000000', strokeThickness: 3,
   954→    });
   955→    panel.add(badge);
   956→
   957→    const name = this.add.text(82, cardTop + 24, def.name, {
   958→      fontSize: '17px', color: '#ffffff', fontStyle: 'bold',
   959→      stroke: '#000', strokeThickness: 3,
   960→    });
   961→    panel.add(name);
   962→
   963→    // ✕ 닫기 (헤더 우측)
   964→    const closeX = 200 + CARD_W / 2 - 14;
   965→    const closeY = cardTop + 16;
   966→    const infoBtnBg = this.add.circle(closeX, closeY, 18, 0x000000, 0)
   967→      .setInteractive({ useHandCursor: true });
   968→    const closeTxt = this.add.text(closeX, closeY, '✕', {
   969→      fontSize: '16px', color: '#999999',
   970→    }).setOrigin(0.5);
   971→    infoBtnBg.on('pointerover', () => closeTxt.setColor('#ffffff'));
   972→    infoBtnBg.on('pointerout',  () => closeTxt.setColor('#999999'));
   973→    infoBtnBg.on('pointerup',   () => this.hideInfoPanel());
   974→    panel.add(infoBtnBg);
   975→    panel.add(closeTxt);
   976→
   977→    // 구분선
   978→    panel.add(this.add.rectangle(200, cardTop + HEADER_H - 4, CARD_W - 20, 1, 0x444444));
   979→
   980→    // ── 기본 효과 ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────
   981→    let curY = cardTop + HEADER_H + 4;
   982→
   983→    panel.add(this.add.text(B_LEFT, curY, '🔷 기본 효과', {
   984→      fontSize: '12px', color: '#88bbff', fontStyle: 'bold',
   985→    }));
   986→    curY += 18;
   987→
   988→    const basicText = this.add.text(B_LEFT, curY, def.basicEffect, {
   989→      fontSize: '13px', color: '#eeeeee', wordWrap: { width: WRAP_W },
   990→    });
   991→    panel.add(basicText);
   992→    curY += basicText.height;
   993→
   994→    if (awakeBonusText) {
   995→      curY += 8;
   996→      panel.add(this.add.text(B_LEFT, curY, awakeBonusText, {
   997→        fontSize: '12px', color: '#ffd700', wordWrap: { width: WRAP_W },
   998→      }));
   999→      curY += (abHeight - 8);
  1000→    }
  1001→
  1002→    curY += 14;
  1003→
  1004→    // ── 특수 능력 ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  1005→    panel.add(this.add.text(B_LEFT, curY, '⚡ 특수 능력', {
  1006→      fontSize: '12px', color: '#ffdd88', fontStyle: 'bold',
  1007→    }));
  1008→    curY += 18;
  1009→
  1010→    panel.add(this.add.text(B_LEFT, curY, def.specialAbility, {
  1011→      fontSize: '13px',
  1012→      color: def.specialAbility === '없음' ? '#666666' : '#eeeeee',
  1013→      wordWrap: { width: WRAP_W },
  1014→    }));
  1015→  }
  1016→
  1017→  private hideInfoPanel(): void {
  1018→    this.infoPanel?.destroy();
  1019→    this.infoPanel = null;
  1020→  }
  1021→
  1022→  private createBackButton() {
  1023→    const btn = this.add.rectangle(200, 573, 200, 50, 0x333333);
  1024→    btn.setStrokeStyle(2, 0x888888);

  1026→    this.add.text(200, 573, '← 돌아가기', {

  1028→      color: '#ffffff',
  1029→      fontStyle: 'bold',
  1030→    }).setOrigin(0.5);
  1031→
  1032→    btn.setInteractive({ useHandCursor: true });

> AGENT

문제를 파악했습니다. 모바일 터치에서 `touchend` → `mouseup` 이벤트가 연속으로 발생하면서, 패널을 여는 탭과 패널 내부 버튼 탭이 동시에 처리되는 것입니다.

`★ Insight ─────────────────────────────────────`
**모바일 터치 고스트 이벤트 문제:**
- 터치 시 브라우저는 `touchend` → ~50ms 후 → `mouseup` 순서로 이벤트를 발생시킴
- 첫 번째 `pointerup`(touchend)이 카드 탭 → 상세 패널 생성
- 두 번째 `pointerup`(mouseup 에뮬레이션)이 새로 생긴 패널의 일러스트/오버레이에 전달
- **BaseScene과 동일한 패턴을 패널 전환에도 적용하면 해결**
`─────────────────────────────────────────────────`

3개 패널 열기 메서드에 입력 가드를 추가합니다.

> DEVELOPER

그리고 캐릭터 목록 화면에서 살짝 밑으로 내린상태에서 배경화면 버튼을 누르면 그 밑에 있는 캐릭터가 눌리는거 같아 배경화면 탭으로 가는게 아니라. 배경화면에서도 역시 그렇고

> AGENT

문제를 이해했습니다. 스크롤된 카드들의 히트박스가 마스크 영역 밖으로 나가도 입력을 계속 받기 때문에, 탭 버튼과 겹치면 카드가 우선 처리됩니다.

`★ Insight ─────────────────────────────────────`
**Phaser 마스크는 렌더링만 가림, 입력은 안 가림:**
- `cardsContainer`에 적용된 mask는 시각적으로만 클리핑
- 스크롤로 올라간 카드의 interactive 영역은 여전히 탭 버튼 위치에 존재
- Phaser의 `topOnly` 기본값 = true → depth가 높은 오브젝트만 이벤트 수신
- **해결: 고정 UI(탭 버튼, 헤더, 뒤로가기)를 cardsContainer보다 높은 depth로 설정**
`─────────────────────────────────────────────────`
